We then show that these learned temporal reasoning models enable us to curate the largest slow-motion video dataset to date from noisy in-the-wild sources. Such slow-motion footage, typically filmed by high-speed cameras, contains substantially richer temporal detail than standard videos. Using this data, we further develop models capable of temporal control, including speed-conditioned video generation, which produces motion at specified playback speed, and temporal super-resolution, which tranforms low-FPS, blurry videos into high-FPS sequences with fine-grained temporal details. Our findings highlight time as a manipulable, perceptual dimension in video learning, opening doors to temporally controllable video generation, temporal forensics detection, and potentially richer world-models that understand how events unfold over time.

</details>


### [107] [Back to Source: Open-Set Continual Test-Time Adaptation via Domain Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21772)
*Yingkai Yang,Chaoqi Chen,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DOCO框架，用于解决开放集持续测试时适应（OCTTA）问题，通过动态样本分割、域补偿提示和结构保持正则化，在持续变化的域和未知语义类别同时出现的情况下，协同完成域适应和OOD检测，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法无法应对同时存在持续域漂移和未知语义类别的现实场景（OCTTA），域偏移和语义偏移的耦合会导致特征空间坍塌，严重降低分类和OOD检测性能。

Method: 提出DOCO框架：1）动态适应条件样本分割，分离可能ID和OOD样本；2）仅用ID样本学习域补偿提示，通过特征统计对齐和结构保持正则化防止语义失真；3）将学到的提示传播到同批次OOD样本，隔离其语义新颖性以提升检测可靠性。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准上，DOCO优于先前的CTTA和OSTTA方法，在OCTTA设定下达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: DOCO通过轻量级的闭环协同机制，有效解决了域适应和OOD检测在持续测试时适应中的耦合问题，为实际场景下的模型鲁棒性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) aims to mitigate distributional shifts between training and test domains during inference time. However, existing TTA methods fall short in the realistic scenario where models face both continually changing domains and the simultaneous emergence of unknown semantic classes, a challenging setting we term Open-set Continual Test-Time Adaptation (OCTTA). The coupling of domain and semantic shifts often collapses the feature space, severely degrading both classification and out-of-distribution detection. To tackle this, we propose DOmain COmpensation (DOCO), a lightweight and effective framework that robustly performs domain adaptation and OOD detection in a synergistic, closed loop. DOCO first performs dynamic, adaptation-conditioned sample splitting to separate likely ID from OOD samples. Then, using only the ID samples, it learns a domain compensation prompt by aligning feature statistics with the source domain, guided by a structural preservation regularizer that prevents semantic distortion. This learned prompt is then propagated to the OOD samples within the same batch, effectively isolating their semantic novelty for more reliable detection. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DOCO outperforms prior CTTA and OSTTA methods, establishing a new state-of-the-art for the demanding OCTTA setting.

</details>


### [108] [Reshoot-Anything: A Self-Supervised Model for In-the-Wild Video Reshooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21776)
*Avinash Paliwal,Adithya Iyer,Shivin Yadav,Muhammad Ali Afridi,Midhun Harikumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督框架，通过从互联网规模单目视频中生成伪多视图训练三元组（源视频、几何锚点、目标视频），实现动态场景的精确相机控制和新视角合成，无需配对多视图数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态视频重拍需要精确的相机控制，但非刚性场景的配对多视图数据极度稀缺，限制了现有方法的可扩展性。

Method: 1) 从单目视频中提取平滑随机游走裁剪轨迹生成源视图和目标视图；2) 利用密集跟踪场前向扭曲源视频首帧合成几何锚点；3) 独立裁剪策略引入空间错位和人工遮挡，迫使模型学习4D时空结构，从源视频跨时间、跨视角路由和重投影缺失纹理以重建目标视图。

Result: 在复杂动态场景上实现最先进的时间一致性、鲁棒的相机控制和高保真新视角合成。

Conclusion: 通过自监督伪多视图训练策略，有效克服了配对数据稀缺瓶颈，使得利用互联网规模单目视频进行动态场景新视角合成成为可能。

Abstract: Precise camera control for reshooting dynamic videos is bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of paired multi-view data for non-rigid scenes. We overcome this limitation with a highly scalable self-supervised framework capable of leveraging internet-scale monocular videos. Our core contribution is the generation of pseudo multi-view training triplets, consisting of a source video, a geometric anchor, and a target video. We achieve this by extracting distinct smooth random-walk crop trajectories from a single input video to serve as the source and target views. The anchor is synthetically generated by forward-warping the first frame of the source with a dense tracking field, which effectively simulates the distorted point-cloud inputs expected at inference. Because our independent cropping strategy introduces spatial misalignment and artificial occlusions, the model cannot simply copy information from the current source frame. Instead, it is forced to implicitly learn 4D spatiotemporal structures by actively routing and re-projecting missing high-fidelity textures across distinct times and viewpoints from the source video to reconstruct the target. At inference, our minimally adapted diffusion transformer utilizes a 4D point-cloud derived anchor to achieve state-of-the-art temporal consistency, robust camera control, and high-fidelity novel view synthesis on complex dynamic scenes.

</details>


### [109] [From Codebooks to VLMs: Evaluating Automated Visual Discourse Analysis for Climate Change on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21786)
*Katharina Prasse,Steffen Jung,Isaac Bravo,Stefanie Walter,Patrick Knab,Christian Bartelt,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了计算机视觉方法（特别是可提示视觉语言模型和零样本CLIP模型）在社交媒体气候传播话语分析中的应用，通过对X平台（原Twitter）上超过120万张图像的标注分析发现，Gemini-3.1-flash-lite模型在所有超类别和数据集上表现最佳，分布级评估显示即使每图准确率中等，VLM预测仍能可靠恢复总体趋势，且链式思维推理反而降低性能，而针对特定标注维度的提示设计可提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体已成为气候传播的主要平台，产生了海量图像和帖子，系统分析这些内容可以揭示哪些传播策略能有效激发公众对气候变化的关注。作者旨在通过分析计算机视觉方法如何用于社交媒体话语分析，来促进这类研究。

Method: 基于应用的需求进行分类体系设计、模型选择、提示工程和验证。对六种可提示视觉语言模型和15种零样本CLIP模型进行了基准测试，使用来自X平台的两个数据集（1,038张专家标注图像和超过120万张图像的大规模语料库），覆盖五个标注维度：动物内容、气候变化后果、气候行动、图像场景和图像类型。

Result: Gemini-3.1-flash-lite在所有超类别和两个数据集上均优于其他模型；开源中等规模模型与顶尖模型差距较小；分布级评估显示VLM预测即使每图准确率中等也能可靠恢复总体趋势；链式思维推理会降低性能；针对特定标注维度设计提示可提升性能。

Conclusion: 可提示视觉语言模型（特别是Gemini-3.1-flash-lite）可用于社交媒体气候话语的规模化分析；推荐采用分布级评估而非仅关注实例级指标；提示设计应针对具体标注维度进行优化，避免使用链式思维推理。

Abstract: Social media platforms have become primary arenas for climate communication, generating millions of images and posts that - if systematically analysed - can reveal which communication strategies mobilise public concern and which fall flat. We aim to facilitate such research by analysing how computer vision methods can be used for social media discourse analysis. This analysis includes application-based taxonomy design, model selection, prompt engineering, and validation. We benchmark six promptable vision-language models and 15 zero-shot CLIP-like models on two datasets from X (formerly Twitter) - a 1,038-image expert-annotated set and a larger corpus of over 1.2 million images, with 50,000 labels manually validated - spanning five annotation dimensions: animal content, climate change consequences, climate action, image setting, and image type. Among the models benchmarked, Gemini-3.1-flash-lite outperforms all others across all super-categories and both datasets, while the gap to open-weight models of moderate size remains relatively small. Beyond instance-level metrics, we advocate for distributional evaluation: VLM predictions can reliably recover population level trends even when per-image accuracy is moderate, making them a viable starting point for discourse analysis at scale. We find that chain-of-thought reasoning reduces rather than improves performance, and that annotation dimension specific prompt design improves performance. We release tweet IDs and labels along with our code at https://github.com/KathPra/Codebooks2VLMs.git.

</details>


### [110] [TEMA: Anchor the Image, Follow the Text for Multi-Modification Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21806)
*Zixu Li,Yupeng Hu,Zhiheng Fu,Zhiwei Chen,Yongqi Li,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对组合图像检索（CIR）中存在的实体覆盖不足和子句-实体对齐问题，构建了多修改数据集M-FashionIQ和M-CIRR，并提出首个支持多修改的CIR框架TEMA（文本导向实体映射架构），在四个基准数据集上验证了其在原始和多修改场景下的优越性及计算效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIR方法依赖简单修改文本，导致两个实际应用中的局限：实体覆盖不足（仅覆盖少量显著变化）和子句-实体对齐问题（修改文本与图像实体不匹配），亟需解决这些问题以推动CIR走向真实场景。

Method: 构建两个富含指令的多修改数据集M-FashionIQ和M-CIRR；提出TEMA（Text-oriented Entity Mapping Architecture），首个支持多修改且兼容简单修改的CIR框架。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，TEMA在原始场景和多修改场景下均具有优越性，同时在检索精度与计算效率之间实现了最优平衡。

Conclusion: TEMA有效解决了现有CIR在实际应用中的两个关键局限，推动了CIR向真实世界使用的进一步发展。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is an important image retrieval paradigm that enables users to retrieve a target image using a multimodal query that consists of a reference image and modification text. Although research on CIR has made significant progress, prevailing setups still rely simple modification texts that typically cover only a limited range of salient changes, which induces two limitations highly relevant to practical applications, namely Insufficient Entity Coverage and Clause-Entity Misalignment. In order to address these issues and bring CIR closer to real-world use, we construct two instruction-rich multi-modification datasets, M-FashionIQ and M-CIRR. In addition, we propose TEMA, the Text-oriented Entity Mapping Architecture, which is the first CIR framework designed for multi-modification while also accommodating simple modifications. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that TEMA's superiority in both original and multi-modification scenarios, while maintaining an optimal balance between retrieval accuracy and computational efficiency. Our codes and constructed multi-modification dataset (M-FashionIQ and M-CIRR) are available at https://github.com/lee-zixu/ACL26-TEMA/.

</details>


### [111] [Multiscale Super Resolution without Image Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21810)
*Daniel Fu,Gabby Litterio,Pedro Felzenszwalb,Rashid Zia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用不同尺度（互质像素尺寸）的低分辨率图像组合来解决超分辨率问题中的歧义性，并展示了通过傅里叶域技术或迭代最小二乘法实现高效重建，同时给出了噪声-分辨率权衡的误差表达式，通过一维和二维实验验证了多尺度超分辨率的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决超分辨率问题在平移操作下的歧义性，即从单尺度低分辨率图像重建高分辨率图像的不适定性。

Method: 利用两两互质的像素尺寸采集多尺度低分辨率图像，使超分辨率问题具有稳定逆解；通过傅里叶域技术或迭代最小二乘法进行高效重建；基于独立同分布噪声假设推导了大信号下最小二乘重建的期望误差表达式。

Result: 一维和二维实验（利用CCD硬件binning实现不同有效像素尺寸）验证了该方法可行；多尺度超分辨率重建在图像质量上优于单尺度方法；理论误差表达式准确描述了噪声-分辨率权衡。

Conclusion: 互质像素尺寸的多尺度成像策略可以有效解决超分辨率的不适定性，为实现高分辨率图像重建提供了理论基础和实用方法，对常见成像系统具有启示意义。

Abstract: We address the ambiguities in the super-resolution problem under translation. We demonstrate that combinations of low-resolution images at different scales can be used to make the super-resolution problem well posed. Such differences in scale can be achieved using sensors with different pixel sizes (as demonstrated here) or by varying the effective pixel size through changes in optical magnification (e.g., using a zoom lens). We show that images acquired with pairwise coprime pixel sizes lead to a system with a stable inverse, and furthermore, that super-resolution images can be reconstructed efficiently using Fourier domain techniques or iterative least squares methods. Our mathematical analysis provides an expression for the expected error of the least squares reconstruction for large signals assuming i.i.d. noise that elucidates the noise-resolution tradeoff. These results are validated through both one- and two-dimensional experiments that leverage charge-coupled device (CCD) hardware binning to explore reconstructions over a large range of effective pixel sizes. Finally, two-dimensional reconstructions for a series of targets are used to demonstrate the advantages of multiscale super-resolution, and implications of these results for common imaging systems are discussed.

</details>


### [112] [Grounding Video Reasoning in Physical Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21873)
*Alibay Osmanli,Zixu Cheng,Shaogang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于物理视频理解的接地基准测试，扩展了V-STaR的“什么-何时-何处”评估结构，涵盖4种视频源、6个物理领域、3种提示族和4种输入条件。实验发现物理提示族表现最强，空间接地最弱，提示族鲁棒性具有选择性。建议视频QA推理基准应报告物理接地、提示感知和扰动感知的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型可以基于文本规律正确回答物理事件问题，但无法在时间或空间上准确定位事件。因此需要提出一个接地的物理视频理解基准，评估模型在时间和空间上的实际定位能力，而不仅仅是命名事件是否正确。

Method: 扩展V-STaR的“什么-何时-何处”评估结构，从SSV2、YouCook2、HoloAssist和Roundabout-TAU四个视频源构建1560个基础视频片段，覆盖6个物理领域。每个片段转换为共享接地事件记录，从中派生出三个提示族（physics、vstar_like、neutral_rstr）和四种输入条件（原始、打乱、消融、帧掩码），时间和空间目标在各提示族间共享。

Result: 物理提示族在所有模型中表现最强，vstar_like是最清晰的非物理语义对比，neutral_rstr作为更难的控制模板。提示族鲁棒性具有选择性而非普遍性，扰动增益集中在原本较差的案例上，空间接地在所有设置中最弱。

Conclusion: 视频QA推理基准应在聚合准确率之外，额外报告物理接地、提示感知和扰动感知的诊断指标，以更全面评估模型的实际物理理解能力。

Abstract: Physical video understanding requires more than naming an event correctly. A model can answer a question about pouring, sliding, or collision from textual regularities while still failing to localize the event in time or space. We introduce a grounded benchmark for physical video understanding that extends the what--when--where evaluation structure of V-STaR to four video sources, six physics domains, three prompt families (physics, vstar_like, and neutral_rstr), and four input conditions (original, shuffled, ablated, and frame-masked). The benchmark contains 1,560 base video clips from SSV2, YouCook2, HoloAssist, and Roundabout-TAU. Each clip is first converted into a shared grounded event record, and the three query families are derived from that record. Temporal and spatial targets are shared across prompt families, while the non-physics families use deterministic family-appropriate semantic a_what targets derived from the same record. Across models and prompt families, physics remains the strongest regime overall, vstar_like is the clearest non-physics semantic comparison, and neutral_rstr behaves as a harder templated control. Prompt-family robustness is selective rather than universal, perturbation gains cluster in weak original cases, and spatial grounding is the weakest across settings. These results suggest that video Q&A reasoning benchmarks shall report physically grounded, prompt-aware, and perturbation-aware diagnostics alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [113] [Directional Confusions Reveal Divergent Inductive Biases Through Rate-Distortion Geometry in Human and Machine Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21909)
*Leyla Roksan Caglar,Pedro A. M. Mediano,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 人类与深度视觉模型在分类准确率相近时，其混淆方向性存在系统性差异：人类呈现广泛而弱的非对称性，而深度模型呈现稀疏而强的方向性塌缩。通过率失真几何框架（斜率、曲率、效率）可量化这些归纳偏置，鲁棒训练虽降低全局非对称性但无法复现人类相似性梯度特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示准确率之外人类与深度视觉模型在分类错误模式上的本质差异，并建立可量化的归纳偏置表征方法。

Method: 在12种扰动类型下匹配人类与深度视觉模型在自然图像分类任务上的混淆矩阵，基于率失真（RD）框架提取三个几何特征（斜率β、曲率κ、效率AUC），结合机制仿真实验验证。

Result: 人类混淆非对称性广泛但强度弱；深度模型非对称性稀疏但强度大；鲁棒训练降低全局非对称性却无法恢复人类特征的广度-强度平衡；不同非对称组织方式即使性能匹配也会使RD前沿向相反方向偏移。

Conclusion: 方向性混淆与RD几何可作为分布偏移下归纳偏置的紧凑可解释表征。

Abstract: Humans and modern vision models can reach similar classification accuracy while making systematically different kinds of mistakes - differing not in how often they err, but in who gets mistaken for whom, and in which direction. We show that these directional confusions reveal distinct inductive biases that are invisible to accuracy alone. Using matched human and deep vision model responses on a natural-image categorization task under 12 perturbation types, we quantify asymmetry in confusion matrices and link it to generalization geometry through a Rate-Distortion (RD) framework, summarized by three geometric signatures (slope (beta), curvature (kappa)) and efficiency (AUC). We find that humans exhibit broad but weak asymmetries, whereas deep vision models show sparser, stronger directional collapses. Robustness training reduces global asymmetry but fails to recover the human-like breadth-strength profile of graded similarity. Mechanistic simulations further show that different asymmetry organizations shift the RD frontier in opposite directions, even when matched for performance. Together, these results position directional confusions and RD geometry as compact, interpretable signatures of inductive bias under distribution shift.

</details>


### [114] [Vista4D: Video Reshooting with 4D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21915)
*Kuan Heng Lin,Zhizheng Liu,Pablo Salamanca,Yash Kant,Ryan Burgert,Yuancheng Xu,Koichi Namekata,Yiwei Zhao,Bolei Zhou,Micah Goldblum,Paul Debevec,Ning Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vista4D 是一个基于4D点云的视频重拍摄框架，通过静态像素分割和4D重建构建4D点云表示，支持对动态视频从新视角和相机轨迹进行重合成，在4D一致性、相机控制和视觉质量上优于现有方法，并可用于动态场景扩展和4D场景重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重拍摄方法在处理真实世界动态视频时，存在深度估计伪影、内容外观保持不足以及新相机轨迹控制不精确等问题。

Method: 构建4D点云表示：通过静态像素分割和4D重建显式保留已见内容并提供丰富相机信号；使用重建的多视角动态数据训练，增强对点云伪影的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多种视频和相机路径下，相比现有基线方法，Vista4D在4D一致性、相机控制和视觉质量上均有提升，且能泛化到动态场景扩展和4D场景重组等实际应用。

Conclusion: Vista4D通过4D点云驱动的显式表示有效解决了视频重拍摄中的关键挑战，实现了更鲁棒和灵活的合成效果。

Abstract: We present Vista4D, a robust and flexible video reshooting framework that grounds the input video and target cameras in a 4D point cloud. Specifically, given an input video, our method re-synthesizes the scene with the same dynamics from a different camera trajectory and viewpoint. Existing video reshooting methods often struggle with depth estimation artifacts of real-world dynamic videos, while also failing to preserve content appearance and failing to maintain precise camera control for challenging new trajectories. We build a 4D-grounded point cloud representation with static pixel segmentation and 4D reconstruction to explicitly preserve seen content and provide rich camera signals, and we train with reconstructed multiview dynamic data for robustness against point cloud artifacts during real-world inference. Our results demonstrate improved 4D consistency, camera control, and visual quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines under a variety of videos and camera paths. Moreover, our method generalizes to real-world applications such as dynamic scene expansion and 4D scene recomposition. See our project page for results, code, and models: https://eyeline-labs.github.io/Vista4D

</details>


### [115] [Context Unrolling in Omni Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21921)
*Ceyuan Yang,Zhijie Lin,Yang Zhao,Fei Xiao,Hao He,Qi Zhao,Chaorui Deng,Kunchang Li,Zihan Ding,Yuwei Guo,Fuyun Wang,Fangqi Zhu,Xiaonan Nie,Shenhan Zhu,Shanchuan Lin,Hongsheng Li,Weilin Huang,Guang Shi,Haoqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Omni是一个统一多模态模型，原生训练于文本、图像、视频、3D几何和隐藏表示等多种模态，通过"上下文展开"机制跨模态推理，聚合互补信息以更忠实地逼近共享多模态知识流形，在多模态生成和理解基准上表现优异，并展示出高级多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏一个真正统一的、原生训练于多种模态（包括隐藏表示）的模态模型，难以实现跨模态互补信息的有效聚合和共享知识流形的忠实逼近。

Method: 提出Omni统一多模态模型，原生训练于文本、图像、视频、3D几何和隐藏表示等多种模态；利用"Context Unrolling（上下文展开）"机制，在生成预测前显式跨多种模态表示进行推理，从而聚合异构模态的互补信息。

Result: 在多模态生成和理解基准上取得强性能，并展示出高级多模态推理能力，包括文本、图像、视频和3D几何的上下文内生成。

Conclusion: Omni通过原生多模态训练和上下文展开机制，有效逼近了共享多模态知识流形，提升了下游推理的保真度，并为统一多模态模型提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We present Omni, a unified multimodal model natively trained on diverse modalities, including text, images, videos, 3D geometry, and hidden representations. We find that such training enables Context Unrolling, where the model explicitly reasons across multiple modal representations before producing predictions. This process enables the model to aggregate complementary information across heterogeneous modalities, facilitating a more faithful approximation of the shared multimodal knowledge manifold and improving downstream reasoning fidelity. As a result, Omni achieves strong performance on both multimodal generation and understanding benchmarks, while demonstrating advanced multimodal reasoning capabilities, including in-context generation of text, image, video, and 3D geometry.

</details>


### [116] [Seeing Without Eyes: 4D Human-Scene Understanding from Wearable IMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21926)
*Hao-Yu Hsu,Tianhang Cheng,Jing Wen,Alexander G. Schwing,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMU-to-4D框架，仅利用可穿戴惯性传感器（如耳机、手表、手机中的IMU）即可重建人体4D运动（3D运动+时间）和粗略3D场景布局，无需视觉输入。通过将大语言模型用于非视觉时空理解，该方法在多种数据集上优于现有级联管线，生成更连贯稳定的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于视觉的人体活动理解在隐私、安全、能耗和可扩展性方面存在根本性挑战，亟需探索无需摄像头的替代感知方案。

Method: 提出IMU-to-4D框架，使用少量来自耳塞、手表或智能手机的惯性传感器数据，将大语言模型重新用于非视觉的时空理解，同时预测详细的人体4D运动和粗略场景结构。

Result: 在多种人-场景数据集上的实验表明，IMU-to-4D相比现有最先进的级联管线能够生成更连贯、时间上更稳定的4D重建结果。

Conclusion: 仅凭可穿戴运动传感器即可支持丰富的4D场景理解，为不依赖视觉的感知任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Understanding human activities and their surrounding environments typically relies on visual perception, yet cameras pose persistent challenges in privacy, safety, energy efficiency, and scalability. We explore an alternative: 4D perception without vision. Its goal is to reconstruct human motion and 3D scene layouts purely from everyday wearable sensors. For this we introduce IMU-to-4D, a framework that repurposes large language models for non-visual spatiotemporal understanding of human-scene dynamics. IMU-to-4D uses data from a few inertial sensors from earbuds, watches, or smartphones and predicts detailed 4D human motion together with coarse scene structure. Experiments across diverse human-scene datasets show that IMU-to-4D yields more coherent and temporally stable results than SoTA cascaded pipelines, suggesting wearable motion sensors alone can support rich 4D understanding.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Sensitivity Uncertainty Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20903)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Harshit R. Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出敏感性-不确定性对齐（SUA）框架，通过衡量预测不稳定性和模型不确定性之间的差异来分析大模型在对抗性和模糊输入下的失败，并给出训练方法和弃权规则以提高模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在对抗性和模糊输入下存在失败行为，对抗敏感性和模糊性反映了同一个根本问题：预测不稳定与模型不确定性之间的错位。现有方法如熵或自一致性无法有效识别这类失败。

Method: 定义标量分数SUA_theta(x)捕捉分布敏感性与预测熵之间的差异；理论证明最小化其正部可约束最坏扰动风险并与校准误差相关；提出SUA-TR训练方法，结合一致性正则化和熵对齐，并设计弃权规则用于安全推理。

Result: 在问答和分类等任务上，SUA比单独使用熵或自一致性更能有效识别模型失败。

Conclusion: SUA框架是模型无关的，为提升不断演进的语言模型的可靠性提供了基础。

Abstract: We propose Sensitivity-Uncertainty Alignment (SUA), a framework for analyzing failures of large language models under adversarial and ambiguous inputs. We argue that adversarial sensitivity and ambiguity reflect a common issue: misalignment between prediction instability and model uncertainty. A reliable model should express higher uncertainty when its predictions are unstable; failure to do so leads to miscalibration.
  We define a scalar score, SUA_theta(x), capturing the difference between distributional sensitivity and predictive entropy. We show that minimizing its positive part bounds worst-case perturbed risk and relates to calibration error. We also formalize ambiguity collapse, where models produce overconfident outputs despite multiple valid interpretations.
  We introduce SUA-TR, a training method combining consistency regularization and entropy alignment, along with an abstention rule for safer inference. Across tasks including question answering and classification, SUA better identifies model failures than entropy or self-consistency alone.
  The framework is model-agnostic and provides a basis for improving reliability in evolving language models.

</details>


### [118] [Omission Constraints Decay While Commission Constraints Persist in Long-Context LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20911)
*Yeran Gamage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM智能体在长对话中，系统提示中的禁止型约束（如不泄露凭证）会随上下文压力逐渐失效，而要求型约束保持稳定，作者称之为安全-召回差异（SRD）。实验表明，在12个模型和8个提供商的4416次试验中，省略合规率从第5轮的73%下降到第16轮的33%，而执行合规率保持100%。在约束失效前重新注入可恢复合规性，且标准监控无法发现此类失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM智能体依赖系统提示中的行为策略（如禁止泄露凭证、禁止数据外泄等）来确保安全。然而，安全评估假设这些约束在整个对话中持续有效，但实际场景中长对话上下文压力可能导致约束衰减，且不同类型约束可能存在不对称性，这一现象尚未被系统研究。

Method: 设计了三臂因果研究，包含4416次试验，覆盖12个模型和8个提供商，在6种对话深度下测试。通过对比省略合规（禁止型约束）与执行合规（要求型约束）的衰减差异，并使用token匹配的填充控制来分离模式语义内容的影响。定义安全转向深度（STD）并在该深度前重新注入约束以验证恢复效果。

Result: 省略合规率从第5轮的73%下降到第16轮的33%，而执行合规率保持100%（Mistral Large 3, p<10⁻³³）。模式语义内容解释了62-100%的稀释效应。在安全转向深度前重新注入约束可恢复合规性而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 生产安全策略中的禁止型约束在长对话中会不可见地失效，而执行型约束保持健康，导致标准监控无法发现安全隐患。需要在安全转向深度前主动重新注入约束来维持安全策略的有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed in production operate under operator-defined behavioral policies (system-prompt instructions such as prohibitions on credential disclosure, data exfiltration, and unauthorized output) that safety evaluations assume hold throughout a conversation. Prohibition-type constraints decay under context pressure while requirement-type constraints persist; we term this asymmetry Security-Recall Divergence (SRD). In a 4,416-trial three-arm causal study across 12 models and 8 providers at six conversation depths, omission compliance falls from 73% at turn 5 to 33% at turn 16 while commission compliance holds at 100% (Mistral Large 3, $p < 10^{-33}$). In the two models with token-matched padding controls, schema semantic content accounts for 62-100% of the dilution effect. Re-injecting constraints before the per-model Safe Turn Depth (STD) restores compliance without retraining. Production security policies consist of prohibitions such as never revealing credentials, never executing untrusted code, and never forwarding user data. Commission-type audit signals remain healthy while omission constraints have already failed, leaving the failure invisible to standard monitoring.

</details>


### [119] [Hidden Secrets in the arXiv: Discovering, Analyzing, and Preventing Unintentional Information Disclosure in Source Files of Scientific Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20927)
*Jan Pennekamp,Johannes Lohmöller,David Schütte,Joscha Loos,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了arXiv上270万份提交的源文件，发现几乎所有提交都包含某种形式的"隐藏"信息（如不必要的文件、元数据、无关内容如注释），包括内部协调链接、API密钥、私钥和完整Git历史等敏感信息，并指出现有清理工具不可靠，提出了ALC-NG解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv公开论文的LaTeX源码等文件可能导致敏感信息意外泄露，但此前缺乏系统性的量化研究，作者希望系统性地回答"有多少敏感内容通过源码被无意泄露"这一问题。

Method: 对270万份有可用源文件的arXiv提交进行大规模分析，从三个维度考察信息泄露：(1)包含不必要文件，(2)文件中嵌入的元数据，(3)文件中无关内容如源代码注释。

Result: 几乎每份arXiv提交都包含某种形式的"隐藏"信息，包括内部协调的可编辑网页链接、API密钥、私钥、完整Git历史等敏感信息。

Conclusion: 现有清理工具无法可靠地去除这些敏感信息，作者提出了ALC-NG工具来全面移除编译LaTeX论文不需要的文件、元数据和注释。

Abstract: Preprints are essential for the timely and open dissemination of research. arXiv, the most widely used preprint service, takes the idea of open science one step further by not only publishing the actual preprints but also LaTeX sources and other files used to create them. As known from other contexts, such as GitHub repositories, and anecdotally exemplified for arXiv, making source code publicly available risks disclosing otherwise "hidden" information. Consequently, the public availability of paper sources raises the question of how much sensitive content is (unintentionally) disclosed through them.
  In this paper, we systematically answer this question for all 2.7M arXiv submissions with available source files across three dimensions of source file-induced information disclosure: (1) inclusion of unnecessary files, (2) metadata embedded in files, and (3) irrelevant content in files such as source code comments. Our analysis reveals that nearly every arXiv submission contains some form of "hidden" information. Notable findings range from links to editable web documents for internal coordination over API and private keys to complete Git histories.
  While different tools promise to remove such information from source files, we show that they fail to reliably achieve the intended cleaning functionality. To mitigate this situation, we provide ALC-NG to comprehensively remove files, metadata, and comments that are not needed to compile a LaTeX paper.

</details>


### [120] [SafeRedirect: Defeating Internal Safety Collapse via Task-Completion Redirection in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20930)
*Chao Pan,Yu Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeRedirect是一种系统级防御方法，通过重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非压制它，有效解决前沿LLM在合法专业任务中自发产生有害内容的内部安全崩溃（ISC）问题，将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM在执行结构上需要有害内容的合法专业任务时，会出现内部安全崩溃（ISC），即自发产生有害内容且安全失败率超过95%。现有输入级防御对此完全无效（100%失败率），而标准系统提示防御只能提供部分缓解。

Method: 提出SafeRedirect系统级覆盖方法，核心思想是重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非压制它。具体包括：授予显式失败许可、规定确定性的硬停止输出、指示模型保留有害占位符未解决状态。

Result: 在7个前沿LLM、3种AI/ML相关ISC任务类型的单轮对话设置中评估，SafeRedirect将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%，远超最强可行基线的55.0%。多模型消融实验显示失败许可和条件特异性是普遍关键组件。跨攻击评估证实对ISC具有最先进防御性能，对其他攻击族的泛化性能至少与基线持平。

Conclusion: SafeRedirect通过重定向而非压制模型驱动力，为ISC问题提供了有效的系统级防御方案，在多个前沿模型上验证了其优越性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Internal Safety Collapse (ISC) is a failure mode in which frontier LLMs, when executing legitimate professional tasks whose correct completion structurally requires harmful content, spontaneously generate that content with safety failure rates exceeding 95%. Existing input-level defenses achieve a 100% failure rate against ISC, and standard system prompt defenses provide only partial mitigation. We propose SafeRedirect, a system-level override that defeats ISC by redirecting the model's task-completion drive rather than suppressing it. SafeRedirect grants explicit permission to fail the task, prescribes a deterministic hard-stop output, and instructs the model to preserve harmful placeholders unresolved. Evaluated on seven frontier LLMs across three AI/ML-related ISC task types in the single-turn setting, SafeRedirect reduces average unsafe generation rates from 71.2% to 8.0%, compared to 55.0% for the strongest viable baseline. Multi-model ablation reveals that failure permission and condition specificity are universally critical, while the importance of other components varies across models. Cross-attack evaluation confirms state-of-the-art defense against ISC with generalization performance at least on par with the baseline on other attack families. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/SafeRedirect.

</details>


### [121] [Adaptive Defense Orchestration for RAG: A Sentinel-Strategist Architecture against Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20932)
*Pranav Pallerla,Wilson Naik Bhukya,Bharath Vemula,Charan Ramtej Kodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，通过自适应、查询感知的防御策略在RAG系统中显著降低安全性与实用性之间的权衡，在消除成员推理攻击的同时将上下文召回率恢复到接近无防御基线的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在医疗、法律等敏感领域依赖私有领域知识，面临成员推理、数据投毒和内容泄露等安全风险。简单启用所有防御会造成严重效用损失（上下文召回率下降超过40%），需要一种能平衡安全与效用的自适应防御机制。

Method: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，包含哨兵（Sentinel）检测异常检索行为，策略师（Strategist）根据查询上下文选择性部署防御。采用自适应防御编排（ADO）方法，仅在必要时激活防御。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和五个编排模型上的实验表明：ADO完全消除了MBA风格的成员推理泄露；在数据投毒攻击下，最强ADO变体将攻击成功率降至接近零，上下文召回率恢复到无防御基线的75%以上（但对模型选择敏感）。

Conclusion: 自适应、查询感知的防御策略能大幅降低RAG系统的安全-效用权衡，相比静态防御栈在保持安全性的同时显著恢复检索效用。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare and law, where they rely on private, domain-specific knowledge. This capability introduces significant security risks, including membership inference, data poisoning, and unintended content leakage. A straightforward mitigation is to enable all relevant defenses simultaneously, but doing so incurs a substantial utility cost. In our experiments, an always-on defense stack reduces contextual recall by more than 40%, indicating that retrieval degradation is the primary failure mode. To mitigate this trade-off in RAG systems, we propose the Sentinel-Strategist architecture, a context-aware framework for risk analysis and defense selection. A Sentinel detects anomalous retrieval behavior, after which a Strategist selectively deploys only the defenses warranted by the query context. Evaluated across three benchmark datasets and five orchestration models, ADO is shown to eliminate MBA-style membership inference leakage while substantially recovering retrieval utility relative to a fully static defense stack, approaching undefended baseline levels. Under data poisoning, the strongest ADO variants reduce attack success to near zero while restoring contextual recall to more than 75% of the undefended baseline, although robustness remains sensitive to model choice. Overall, these findings show that adaptive, query-aware defense can substantially reduce the security-utility trade-off in RAG systems.

</details>


### [122] [Breaking Bad: Interpretability-Based Safety Audits of State-of-the-Art LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20945)
*Krishiv Agarwal,Ramneet Kaur,Colin Samplawski,Manoj Acharya,Anirban Roy,Daniel Elenius,Brian Matejek,Adam D. Cobb,Susmit Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性驱动的红队攻击框架，通过对八个开源大语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Llama-3.3-70B-4bt、GPT-oss-20B、GPT-oss-120B、Qwen3-0.6B、Qwen3-32B、Phi4-3.8B、Phi4-14B）进行激活操控攻击（Universal Steering 和 Representation Engineering），并引入自适应两阶段网格搜索算法寻找最优激活操控系数。结果显示Llama-3系列模型高度脆弱（最高91%破解率），GPT-oss-120B最为鲁棒，不同大小的Qwen和Phi模型表现差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全审计多采用黑盒探针方法，缺乏对模型内部机制的系统性漏洞挖掘工具，亟需可解释性驱动的审计方法以深入理解模型安全缺陷。

Method: 采用两种可解释性方法（Universal Steering和Representation Engineering）对八个开源LLM进行激活操控；提出自适应两阶段网格搜索算法以确定最优激活操控系数；使用精心挑选的有害查询集和基于LLM的标准化评判协议进行评估。

Result: Llama-3.3-70B-4bt在US方法下破解率高达91%，RepE方法下83%；GPT-oss-120B对两种方法均表现鲁棒；小尺寸Qwen3-0.6B和Phi4-3.8B破解率较低，而大尺寸对应模型更易受影响；模型间安全鲁棒性差异显著。

Conclusion: 基于可解释性的激活操控是进行系统性安全审计的有效工具，但同时也凸显了其双重用途风险，LLM部署需要更强的内部防御机制。

Abstract: Effective safety auditing of large language models (LLMs) demands tools that go beyond black-box probing and systematically uncover vulnerabilities rooted in model internals. We present a comprehensive, interpretability-driven jailbreaking audit of eight SOTA open-source LLMs: Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, GPT-oss- 20B, GPT-oss-120B, Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-32B, Phi4-3.8B, and Phi4-14B. Leveraging interpretability-based approaches -- Universal Steering (US) and Representation Engineering (RepE) -- we introduce an adaptive two-stage grid search algorithm to identify optimal activation-steering coefficients for unsafe behavioral concepts. Our evaluation, conducted on a curated set of harmful queries and a standardized LLM-based judging protocol, reveals stark contrasts in model robustness. The Llama-3 models are highly vulnerable, with up to 91\% (US) and 83\% (RepE) jailbroken responses on Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, while GPT-oss-120B remains robust to attacks via both interpretability approaches. Qwen and Phi models show mixed results, with the smaller Qwen3-0.6B and Phi4-3.8B mostly exhibiting lower jailbreaking rates, while their larger counterparts are more susceptible. Our results establish interpretability-based steering as a powerful tool for systematic safety audits, but also highlight its dual-use risks and the need for better internal defenses in LLM deployment.

</details>


### [123] [Breaking MCP with Function Hijacking Attacks: Novel Threats for Function Calling and Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20994)
*Yannis Belkhiter,Giulio Zizzo,Sergio Maffeis,Seshu Tirupathi,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对智能体AI模型的新型函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操纵工具选择过程强制调用攻击者指定的函数。该攻击对语义上下文不敏感、对函数集鲁棒，可生成通用对抗函数，在5种模型上达到70%-100%的攻击成功率（ASR），揭示了智能体系统需要更强的安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已探索LLM在用户提示操纵下的注入与越狱攻击漏洞，但智能体模型通过函数调用接口引入了新的攻击面。虽然已有工作表明函数调用可被滥用导致数据篡改、死循环或有害内容生成，但尚未有针对工具选择过程的操纵攻击。本文旨在填补这一空白，展示函数劫持攻击的可行性。

Method: 提出函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操纵智能体模型的工具选择过程，强制模型调用攻击者指定的函数。FHA对上下文语义不敏感（语义无关性），对函数集变化具有鲁棒性。进一步提出可训练生成通用对抗函数，使单一攻击函数能够在多个查询和载荷配置下劫持工具选择。

Result: 在5种不同模型（包括指令模型和推理模型）上，基于BFCL数据集进行实验，FHA达到70%到100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。

Conclusion: FHA攻击揭示了智能体系统在函数调用方面的严重安全漏洞，现有安全措施不足，需要更强有力的护栏和安全模块来保护智能体系统免受此类攻击。

Abstract: The growth of agentic AI has drawn significant attention to function calling Large Language Models (LLMs), which are designed to extend the capabilities of AI-powered system by invoking external functions. Injection and jailbreaking attacks have been extensively explored to showcase the vulnerabilities of LLMs to user prompt manipulation. The expanded capabilities of agentic models introduce further vulnerabilities via their function calling interface. Recent work in LLM security showed that function calling can be abused, leading to data tampering and theft, causing disruptive behavior such as endless loops, or causing LLMs to produce harmful content in the style of jailbreaking attacks. This paper introduces a novel function hijacking attack (FHA) that manipulates the tool selection process of agentic models to force the invocation of a specific, attacker-chosen function. While existing attacks focus on semantic preference of the model for function-calling tasks, we show that FHA is largely agnostic to the context semantics and robust to the function sets, making it applicable across diverse domains. We further demonstrate that FHA can be trained to produce universal adversarial functions, enabling a single attacked function to hijack tool selection across multiple queries and payload configurations. We conducted experiments on 5 different models, including instructed and reasoning variants, reaching 70% to 100% ASR over the established BFCL dataset. Our findings further demonstrate the need for strong guardrails and security modules for agentic systems.

</details>


### [124] [VRSafe: A Secure Virtual Keyboard to Mitigate Keystroke Inference in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21001)
*Yijun Yuan,Na Du,Adam J. Lee,Balaji Palanisamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VRSafe的新型虚拟现实QWERTY键盘，通过引入假阳性击键和恶意登录检测器，有效防御VR环境中的击键推断攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码认证在VR应用中面临击键推断攻击威胁，而针对VR环境的有效且成本低廉的防御策略尚属空白。

Method: 设计VRSafe虚拟键盘：(1) 在攻击者收集的击键信息中引入假阳性击键，干扰密码推断；(2) 集成恶意登录检测器，识别基于推断凭据的未授权登录尝试。

Result: 仿真实验和用户研究均表明，VRSafe显著降低了击键推断攻击的准确率，且用户体验开销适中。

Conclusion: VRSafe是一种有效且实用的VR击键推断攻击防御方案，兼顾安全性与可用性。

Abstract: Password-based authentication is one of the most commonly used methods for verifying user identities, and its widespread usage continues in virtual reality (VR) applications. As a result, various forms of attacks on password-based authentication in traditional environments such as keystroke inference and shoulder surfing, are still effective in VR applications. While keystroke inference attacks on virtual keyboards have been studied extensively, few efforts have developed an effective and cost-efficient defense strategy to mitigate keystroke inferences in VR. To address this gap, this paper presents a novel QWERTY keyboard called \textit{VRSafe} that is resilient to keystroke inference attacks. The proposed keyboard carefully introduces false positive keystrokes into the information collected by attackers during the typing process, making the inference of the original password difficult. \textit{VRSafe} also incorporates a novel malicious login detector that can effectively identify unauthorized login attempts using credentials inferred from keystroke inference attacks with high detection rate and minimal time and memory cost. The proposed design is evaluated through both simulation experiments and a real-world user study, and the results show that \textit{VRSafe} can significantly reduce the accuracy of keystroke inference attacks while incurring a modest overhead from a usability standpoint.

</details>


### [125] [Layer 2 Blockchains Simplified: A Survey of Vector Commitment Schemes, ZKP Frameworks, Layer-2 Data Structures and Verkle Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21055)
*Ekleen Kaur,Marko Suvajdzic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出统一的安全视角调查，系统性地将L2架构映射到其底层密码学安全基础，分析ZK框架的安全假设（离散对数、CDH、双线性DH）及其承诺方案（KZG、IPA），并形式化涵盖排序器活性、桥接漏洞和数据可用性故障的全面L2威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L2协议在提升吞吐量的同时引入了由链下组件（排序器、桥接、数据可用性机制）构成的复杂安全面，而现有文献对此风险的视角碎片化，缺乏统一的安全分析框架。

Method: 采用结构化分析方法，从L1原语到现代L2核心进行技术演进剖析，系统分析ZK框架的密码学安全假设（离散对数、CDH、双线性DH）及承诺方案（KZG、IPA），并形式化定义涵盖排序器活性、桥接漏洞和数据可用性故障的L2威胁模型。

Result: 构建了首个统一的、以安全为中心的L2调查，将L2架构严格映射到其底层密码学安全基础，并形式化了全面的L2威胁模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究人员和开发者提供了一个可访问且严谨的参考，使其能够从深度密码数学视角推理L2安全性。

Abstract: Layer-2 (L2) protocols address the fundamental limitations of Layer-1 (L1) blockchains by offloading computation while anchoring trust to the parent chain. This architectural shift, while boosting throughput, introduces a new, complex security surface defined by off-chain components like sequencers, bridges, and data availability mechanisms. Prior literature[31][33] offers fragmented views of this risk. This paper presents the first unified, security-focused survey that rigorously maps L2 architecture to its underlying cryptographic security. We dissect the technical progression from L1 primitives to the core of modern L2s, analyzing the security assumptions(Discrete Logarithm, Computational Diffie-Hellman, Bilinear Diffie-Hellman) of ZK frameworks (Groth16, Plonk) and their corresponding commitment schemes (KZG, IPA). We formalize a comprehensive L2 threat model encompassing sequencer liveness, bridge exploits, and data-availability failures. This work serves as an accessible yet rigorous reference for researchers and developers to reason about L2 security from a deep crypto-mathematical perspective.

</details>


### [126] [Behavioral Consistency and Transparency Analysis on Large Language Model API Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21083)
*Guanjie Lin,Yinxin Wan,Shichao Pei,Ting Xu,Kuai Xu,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GateScope是一个轻量级黑盒测量框架，用于评估商业LLM API网关的行为一致性和操作透明度。通过分析10个真实世界的商业网关，发现存在模型降级/替换、静默截断、计费不准确和延迟不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方LLM API网关作为统一访问点正在快速普及，但其内部路由、缓存和计费策略缺乏透明度，用户无法验证请求是否由广告中声称的模型服务、响应是否忠实于上游API、发票是否准确反映公开定价策略。

Method: GateScope从四个关键维度进行审计：响应内容分析、多轮对话性能、计费准确性和延迟特征。采用轻量级黑盒测量方式，无需网关内部信息即可检测关键不当行为。

Result: 对10个商业LLM API网关的测量揭示了实际行为与预期行为之间的频繁差距，包括静默模型替换、记忆保持能力下降、偏离公布定价以及各平台间延迟稳定性的显著差异。

Conclusion: 现有商业LLM API网关普遍存在透明度不足问题，GateScope能够有效检测这些不透明行为，为提升网关服务质量和用户知情权提供技术支持。

Abstract: Third-party Large Language Model (LLM) API gateways are rapidly emerging as unified access points to models offered by multiple vendors. However, the internal routing, caching, and billing policies of these gateways are largely undisclosed, leaving users with limited visibility into whether requests are served by the advertised models, whether responses remain faithful to upstream APIs, or whether invoices accurately reflect public pricing policies. To address this gap, we introduce GateScope, a lightweight black-box measurement framework for evaluating behavioral consistency and operational transparency in commercial LLM gateways. GateScope is designed to detect key misbehaviors, including model downgrading or switching, silent truncation, billing inaccuracies, and instability in latency by auditing gateways along four critical dimensions: response content analysis, multi-turn conversation performance, billing accuracy, and latency characteristics. Our measurements across 10 real-world commercial LLM API gateways reveal frequent gaps between expected and actual behaviors, including silent model substitutions, degraded memory retention, deviations from announced pricing, and substantial variation in latency stability across platforms.

</details>


### [127] [Cross-Session Threats in AI Agents: Benchmark, Evaluation, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21131)
*Ari Azarafrooz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨会话威胁检测任务，构建了CSTM-Bench基准数据集（26种攻击分类、54个场景），通过测量发现会话边界检测器和全日志拼接检测器在跨会话攻击中召回率损失约50%，并提出基于核心集记忆的Coreset Memory Reader（K=50）作为唯一能在所有分片上保持召回率的方法，同时引入CSR_prefix指标用于衡量服务稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体防护机制是"无记忆"的——每条消息被孤立判断，攻击者可将单个攻击分散到数十个会话中，因每个会话单独无害而绕过检测。需要跨会话的威胁检测能力。

Method: (1) 构建CSTM-Bench数据集：26种可执行攻击分类，按杀伤链阶段和跨会话操作分类，附带良性样本；(2) 基于信息瓶颈框架测量跨会话检测性能；(3) 提出Coreset Memory Reader（K=50核心集记忆）进行跨会话证据留存，并引入CSR_prefix指标（有序前缀稳定性，无需LLM）与服务稳定性联合优化。

Result: 会话边界检测器和全日志拼接检测器在从dilution分片到cross_session分片时，攻击召回率均损失约一半。只有Coreset Memory Reader（K=50）能在两个分片上保持召回率。

Conclusion: 跨会话攻击检测需要带记忆的阅读器而非简单拼接或孤立判断，Coreset Memory Reader结合CSR_prefix指标为实际部署提供了兼顾检测性能与服务稳定性的帕累托优化方案。

Abstract: AI-agent guardrails are memoryless: each message is judged in isolation, so an adversary who spreads a single attack across dozens of sessions slips past every session-bound detector because only the aggregate carries the payload. We make three contributions to cross-session threat detection.
  (1) Dataset. CSTM-Bench is 26 executable attack taxonomies classified by kill-chain stage and cross-session operation (accumulate, compose, launder, inject_on_reader), each bound to one of seven identity anchors that ground-truth "violation" as a policy predicate, plus matched Benign-pristine and Benign-hard confounders. Released on Hugging Face as intrinsec-ai/cstm-bench with two 54-scenario splits: dilution (compositional) and cross_session (12 isolation-invisible scenarios produced by a closed-loop rewriter that softens surface phrasing while preserving cross-session artefacts).
  (2) Measurement. Framing cross-session detection as an information bottleneck to a downstream correlator LLM, we find that a session-bound judge and a Full-Log Correlator concatenating every prompt into one long-context call both lose roughly half their attack recall moving from dilution to cross_session, well inside any frontier context window. Scope: 54 scenarios per shard, one correlator family (Anthropic Claude), no prompt optimisation; we release it to motivate larger, multi-provider datasets.
  (3) Algorithm and metric. A bounded-memory Coreset Memory Reader retaining highest-signal fragments at $K=50$ is the only reader whose recall survives both shards. Because ranker reshuffles break KV-cache prefix reuse, we promote $\mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$ (ordered prefix stability, LLM-free) to a first-class metric and fuse it with detection into $\mathrm{CSTM} = 0.7 F_1(\mathrm{CSDA@action}, \mathrm{precision}) + 0.3 \mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$, benchmarking rankers on a single Pareto of recall versus serving stability.

</details>


### [128] [Position Paper: Denial-of-Service Against Multi-Round Transaction Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21169)
*Yuzhe Tang,Yibo Wang,Wanning Ding,Jiaqi Chen,Taesoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对以太坊打包服务（如Flashbots Bundles）的新型拒绝服务攻击，利用交易间依赖关系和打包服务特性（如原子区块包含），实现了隐蔽、零风险、低成本的DoS攻击，实验证明该攻击能显著降低构建者收入并减慢区块生成速度，同时提出了相应的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝服务攻击设计由于打包服务独特的多轮执行模型而失效，因此需要研究针对打包服务的新型不对称拒绝服务攻击。

Method: 利用合约状态的交易间依赖关系实现隐蔽性，滥用打包服务特有的原子区块包含特性来降低攻击的资本和运营成本。

Result: 攻击实现了高成功率，显著降低了构建者的收入，并减慢了区块生成速度。

Conclusion: 针对识别出的风险提出了缓解策略。

Abstract: In Ethereum, transaction-bundling services are a critical component of block builders, such as Flashbots Bundles, and are widely used by MEV searchers. Disrupting bundling services can degrade searcher experience and reduce builder revenue. Despite the extensive studies, the existing denial-of-service attack designs are ineffective against bundling services due to their unique multi-round execution model.
  This paper studies the open problem of asymmetric denial-of-service against bundling services. We develop evasive, risk-free, and low-cost DoS attacks on Flashbots' bundling service, the only open-source bundling service known to us. Our attacks exploit inter-transaction dependencies through contract state to achieve evasiveness, and abuse bundling-specific features, such as atomic block inclusion, to significantly reduce both capital and operational costs of the attack.
  Experimental results show that our attacks achieve high success rates, substantially reduce builders' revenue, and slow block production. We further propose mitigation strategies for the identified risks.

</details>


### [129] [Physically Unclonable Functions for Secure IoT Authentication and Hardware-Anchored AI Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21188)
*Maryam Taghi Zadeh,Mohsen Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了AIoT系统中基于硬件的信任机制，包括TPM、PUF、混合硬件信任根及纯软件方案，比较了它们在安全性、可扩展性、成本和部署复杂度上的权衡，指出PUF和混合信任锚在大规模AIoT场景中更具优势，而纯软件方案在物理暴露和对抗环境下存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI与IoT/边缘计算的深度融合，系统面临设备认证和AI模型完整性保障的迫切需求，尤其是在资源和安全约束严格的场景下，现有信任机制缺乏系统性的比较和评估。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对TPM、PUF、混合容器感知硬件信任根以及纯软件安全方案进行梳理、对比分析，从安全性、可扩展性、成本、部署复杂度等多个维度评估其优劣。

Result: 硬件根信任方案在抗物理篡改和设备克隆方面优于纯软件方案；PUF和混合信任锚在大规模AIoT系统中展现出更优的平衡性；纯软件方案在物理暴露和对抗环境下安全性不足。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了当前信任机制的设计挑战，为未来可信AIoT平台的发展提供了指导方向。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into Internet of Things (IoT) and edge computing systems has intensified the need for robust, hardware-rooted trust mechanisms capable of ensuring device authenticity and AI model integrity under strict resource and security constraints. This survey reviews and synthesizes existing literature on hardware-rooted trust mechanisms for AI-enabled IoT systems. It systematically examines and compares representative trust anchor mechanisms, including Trusted Platform Module (TPM)-based measurement and attestation, silicon and FPGA-based Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs), hybrid container-aware hardware roots of trust, and software-only security approaches. The analysis highlights how hardware-rooted solutions generally provide stronger protection against physical tampering and device cloning compared to software-only approaches, particularly in adversarial and physically exposed environments, while hybrid designs extend hardware trust into runtime and containerized environments commonly used in modern edge deployments. By evaluating trade-offs among security strength, scalability, cost, and deployment complexity, the study shows that PUF-based and hybrid trust anchors offer a promising balance for large-scale, AI-enabled IoT systems, whereas software-only trust mechanisms remain insufficient in adversarial and physically exposed settings. The presented comparison aims to clarify current design challenges and guide future development of trustworthy AI-enabled IoT platforms.

</details>


### [130] [ECCFROG522PP: An Enhanced 522 bit Weierstrass Elliptic Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21261)
*Victor Duarte Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ECCFROG522PP，一条522比特素域椭圆曲线（短Weierstrass形式），所有关键参数均通过BLAKE3确定性管道从公开种子生成，强调透明度、可审计性和可复现性，目标安全等级与P-521相当，具备素数阶、余因子为1、完全阶基点和抗攻击安全验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有椭圆曲线（如NIST P-256、secp256k1、P-521、Curve448、Brainpool P512）在更高安全需求下存在参数生成过程不够透明、不可完全独立复现的问题，本文旨在设计一条完全公开可验证、参数可确定性推导的曲线，以增强信任和审计能力。

Method: 采用BLAKE3哈希函数构建确定性参数生成管道，所有曲线参数（包括素数、系数、基点）均从单一固定公开种子通过指定索引推导得出；对生成的曲线进行素数阶验证、余因子为1验证、基点完整阶验证、二次扭安全验证（大素数因子）、嵌入度下界计算及低嵌入度攻击检查和CM判别式异常检查。

Result: 成功构造了一条522比特素数阶椭圆曲线，余因子为1，基点具有完整阶；二次扭具有大素数因子；提供了嵌入度的可证明下界；通过了低嵌入度约简攻击和CM判别式异常的标准安全检查；完整生成和验证流程可从公开工件和参考脚本端到端复现。

Conclusion: ECCFROG522PP提供了一条与P-521安全等级相当但完全透明可审计的椭圆曲线，其确定性生成过程使所有参数均可独立验证，适合对信任和透明度有高要求的应用场景。

Abstract: This paper presents ECCFROG522PP, a 522-bit prime-field elliptic curve in short Weierstrass form, designed with a focus on deterministic generation and public reproducibility. The central design principle is that all critical parameters are derived from a fixed public seed through a transparent and verifiable procedure. While many deployed systems rely on NIST P-256 and secp256k1, which target approximately 128-bit classical security, higher security applications typically consider curves such as NIST P-521, Curve448, and Brainpool P512. ECCFROG522PP is intended for the same general classical security range as P-521, with emphasis on transparency, auditability, and reproducibility rather than performance optimization.
  The curve parameters are generated through a BLAKE3-based deterministic pipeline with publicly specified indices. The resulting construction has prime order, cofactor one, and a deterministically derived base point of full order. The quadratic twist has a large proven prime factor, and the construction includes a documented lower bound on the embedding degree together with standard sanity checks against low embedding degree reductions and basic CM discriminant anomalies.
  The full generation and validation procedure can be reproduced end to end from public artifacts and reference scripts, enabling independent verification of all parameters and checks.

</details>


### [131] [Strategic Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Architecture for Cost-Effective Code Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21282)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于博弈论启发的异构多智能体架构"3+1"，用于自动化代码漏洞检测。该架构使用三个云端大模型专家（DeepSeek-V3）并行分析代码，结合本地轻量级验证器（Qwen3-8B）进行对抗性验证，在NIST Juliet测试集上以每样本0.002美元的成本达到77.2%的F1分数和100%的召回率，并通过并行执行实现3.0倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化代码漏洞检测方法在检测精度和计算成本之间存在根本性权衡，亟需一种既能保持高检测精度又能控制计算成本的解决方案。

Method: 提出"3+1"异构多智能体架构：三个云端专家智能体（DeepSeek-V3）分别从代码结构、安全模式、调试逻辑三个互补视角并行分析代码，本地轻量级验证器（Qwen3-8B）以零边际成本进行对抗性验证。通过两层博弈框架形式化设计：专家间的合作博弈捕捉多视角的超加性价值，对抗性验证博弈建模质量保证激励机制。

Result: 在NIST Juliet测试集的262个真实样本（涵盖14种CWE类型，均衡的脆弱与良性类别）上，达到77.2%的F1分数、62.9%的精确率、100%的召回率，每样本成本0.002美元。优于单专家LLM基线（F1 71.4%）和Cppcheck静态分析（MCC 0）。对抗性验证器显著提升精确率（+10.3个百分点，p<1e-6，McNemar检验），并行执行实现3.0倍加速。

Conclusion: 证明了博弈论设计原则能够有效指导面向成本敏感的软件工程任务的异构多智能体架构构建，为自动化代码漏洞检测提供了兼顾精度、成本和效率的可行方案。

Abstract: Automated code vulnerability detection is critical for software security, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off between detection accuracy and computational cost. We propose a heterogeneous multi-agent architecture inspired by game-theoretic principles, combining cloud-based LLM experts with a local lightweight verifier. Our "3+1" architecture deploys three cloud-based expert agents (DeepSeek-V3) that analyze code from complementary perspectives - code structure, security patterns, and debugging logic - in parallel, while a local verifier (Qwen3-8B) performs adversarial validation at zero marginal cost.
  We formalize this design through a two-layer game framework: (1) a cooperative game among experts capturing super-additive value from diverse perspectives, and (2) an adversarial verification game modeling quality assurance incentives.
  Experiments on 262 real samples from the NIST Juliet Test Suite across 14 CWE types, with balanced vulnerable and benign classes, demonstrate that our approach achieves a 77.2% F1 score with 62.9% precision and 100% recall at $0.002 per sample - outperforming both a single-expert LLM baseline (F1 71.4%) and Cppcheck static analysis (MCC 0). The adversarial verifier significantly improves precision (+10.3 percentage points, p < 1e-6, McNemar's test) by filtering false positives, while parallel execution achieves a 3.0x speedup.
  Our work demonstrates that game-theoretic design principles can guide effective heterogeneous multi-agent architectures for cost-sensitive software engineering tasks.

</details>


### [132] [Adversarial Evasion in Non-Stationary Malware Detection: Minimizing Drift Signals through Similarity-Constrained Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21310)
*Pawan Acharya,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非平稳环境下，如何生成既能逃避恶意软件分类器检测、又能避开漂移监控机制的对抗性恶意软件样本，通过相似性正则化方法平衡目标误分类与漂移信号最小化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习恶意软件检测模型在非平稳环境中面临持续演变的问题，攻击者可能利用对抗性样本同时逃避分类和漂移监控，但该方向缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在分类器的标准化特征空间中生成目标对抗样本，引入相似性正则化项（如ℓ2正则化）约束扰动，保持与干净恶意软件的分布相似性，构建同时优化目标误分类和漂移信号最小化的损失函数。

Result: 相似性约束能有效降低输出漂移信号，其中ℓ2正则化效果最优；扰动预算显著影响逃逸-可检测性权衡，预算增加会提高攻击成功率但也会产生更强的漂移指标。

Conclusion: 对抗性恶意软件可以在逃避分类的同时规避漂移检测，但需要在攻击成功率和可检测性之间进行权衡，这为设计更鲁棒的恶意软件检测系统提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Deep learning has emerged as a powerful approach for malware detection, demonstrating impressive accuracy across various data representations. However, these models face critical limitations in real-world, non-stationary environments where both malware characteristics and detection systems continuously evolve. Our research investigates a fundamental security question: Can an attacker generate adversarial malware samples that simultaneously evade classification and remain inconspicuous to drift monitoring mechanisms? We propose a novel approach that generates targeted adversarial examples in the classifier's standardized feature space, augmented with sophisticated similarity regularizers. By carefully constraining perturbations to maintain distributional similarity with clean malware, we create an optimization objective that balances targeted misclassification with drift signal minimization. We quantify the effectiveness of this approach by comprehensively comparing classifier output probabilities using multiple drift metrics. Our experiments demonstrate that similarity constraints can reduce output drift signals, with $\ell_2$ regularization showing the most promising results. We observe that perturbation budget significantly influences the evasion-detectability trade-off, with increased budget leading to higher attack success rates and more substantial drift indicators.

</details>


### [133] [Provably Secure Steganography Based on List Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21394)
*Kaiyi Pang,Minhao Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于列表解码的高容量可证明安全隐写方案，通过维护候选集合替代直接搜索正确消息，并引入后缀匹配机制确保正确性，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升嵌入容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可证明安全隐写方案基于语言模型可保证计算不可区分性，但嵌入容量受限。熵利用率低下导致其不适用于大型语言模型（LLM），后者固有的低熵特性严重限制了可行的嵌入容量。

Method: 基于列表解码概念：维护包含正确秘密消息的候选集合而非直接寻找正确消息。引入后缀匹配机制从候选中区分正确秘密消息。提供安全性和正确性的理论证明，并推导理论容量下界。该方法即插即用，仅需替换模型的标准随机采样模块。

Result: 在三个LLM和七个PSS基线上的实验表明，该方法在计算效率与先前PSS方案相当的同时，实现了嵌入容量的显著提升。

Conclusion: 提出了一种理论上证明高容量的可证明安全隐写方案，通过列表解码和后缀匹配有效解决了LLM低熵特性下的容量瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Steganography embeds secret messages in seemingly innocuous carriers for covert communication under surveillance. Current Provably Secure Steganography (PSS) schemes based on language models can guarantee computational indistinguishability between the covertext and stegotext. However, achieving high embedding capacity remains a challenge for existing PSS. The inefficient entropy utilization renders them not well-suited for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose inherent low-entropy tendencies severely constrain feasible embedding capacity. To address this, we propose a provably secure steganography scheme with a theoretically proved high capacity. Our scheme is based on the concept of list decoding: it maintains a set of candidates that contain the correct secret message, instead of directly finding the correct message with more effort. This strategy fully utilizes the information content of the generated text, yielding higher capacity. To ensure the correctness of our scheme, we further introduce a suffix-matching mechanism to distinguish the correct secret message from the candidates. We provide theoretical proofs for both the security and correctness of our scheme, alongside a derivation of its theoretical capacity lower bound.
  Our approach is plug-and-play, requiring only a direct replacement of the model's standard random sampling module. Experiments on three LLMs and seven PSS baselines demonstrate that our method achieves computational efficiency comparable to prior PSS schemes while delivering a substantial improvement in embedding capacity.

</details>


### [134] [CSC: Turning the Adversary's Poison against Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21416)
*Yuchen Shi,Xin Guo,Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC)方法，通过早期特征聚类识别并隐藏中毒样本，将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时保持模型在干净数据上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于毒药抑制的防御方法对特定攻击变种检测不足，且遗忘方法会导致模型准确率严重下降。

Method: CSC包含两个阶段：（1）隔离阶段：标准监督训练后，从早期epoch提取特征，使用DBSCAN聚类，基于类别多样性和密度指标识别异常聚类；（2）隐藏阶段：将被识别的中毒样本重新标记为虚拟类，使用交叉熵损失微调分类器，用良性虚拟关联替代后门关联。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上针对12种投毒攻击进行评估，与9种先进防御相比，CSC将平均攻击成功率降至接近零，且干净准确率损失极小。

Conclusion: CSC能鲁棒地识别后门模式，提供有效的隐藏机制，在实证中优于现有防御，推动了可信人工智能的发展。

Abstract: Poisoning-based backdoor attacks pose significant threats to deep neural networks by embedding triggers in training data, causing models to misclassify triggered inputs as adversary-specified labels while maintaining performance on clean data. Existing poison restraint-based defenses often suffer from inadequate detection against specific attack variants and compromise model utility through unlearning methods that lead to accuracy degradation. This paper conducts a comprehensive analysis of backdoor attack dynamics during model training, revealing that poisoned samples form isolated clusters in latent space early on, with triggers acting as dominant features distinct from benign ones. Leveraging these insights, we propose Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC), a novel poison suppression defense. CSC first trains a deep neural network via standard supervised learning while segregating poisoned samples through feature extraction from early epochs, DBSCAN clustering, and identification of anomalous clusters based on class diversity and density metrics. In the concealment stage, identified poisoned samples are relabeled to a virtual class, and the model's classifier is fine-tuned using cross-entropy loss to replace the backdoor association with a benign virtual linkage, preserving overall accuracy. CSC was evaluated on four benchmark datasets against twelve poisoning-based attacks, CSC outperforms nine state-of-the-art defenses by reducing average attack success rates to near zero with minimal clean accuracy loss. Contributions include robust backdoor patterns identification, an effective concealment mechanism, and superior empirical validation, advancing trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [135] [Differentially Private De-identification of Dutch Clinical Notes: A Comparative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21421)
*Michele Miranda,Xinlan Yan,Nishant Mishra,Rachel Murphy,Ameen Abu-Hanna,Sébastien Bratières,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次比较研究了差分隐私(DP)、命名实体识别(NER)和大语言模型(LLM)在荷兰语临床文本去标识化中的表现，发现单独使用DP会严重损害效用，但与LLM预处理结合可显著改善隐私-效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床文本去标识化是保护患者隐私、实现医疗数据二次利用的关键，但手动去标识化成本高且速度慢，亟需自动化方法。现有研究缺乏对DP、NER和LLM三种方法在临床文本去标识化中的系统比较，尤其是在荷兰语临床文本领域尚属空白。

Method: 本文对DP、NER和LLM三种方法分别进行实验，并探索了NER或LLM预处理后结合DP的混合策略。评估指标包括隐私泄露程度以及外在评估（实体分类和关系分类）表现。

Result: 单独使用DP机制会严重降低数据效用；结合语言预处理（尤其是基于LLM的掩码处理）后再应用DP，能够显著改善隐私与效用之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 将LLM预处理的语义能力与DP的形式化隐私保证相结合，是提升临床文本去标识化性能的有效路径，在隐私保护和数据可用性之间实现了更优平衡。

Abstract: Protecting patient privacy in clinical narratives is essential for enabling secondary use of healthcare data under regulations such as GDPR and HIPAA. While manual de-identification remains the gold standard, it is costly and slow, motivating the need for automated methods that combine privacy guarantees with high utility. Most automated text de-identification pipelines employed named entity recognition (NER) to identify protected entities for redaction. Although methods based on differential privacy (DP) provide formal privacy guarantees, more recently also large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text de-identification in the clinical domain. In this work, we present the first comparative study of DP, NER, and LLMs for Dutch clinical text de-identification. We investigate these methods separately as well as hybrid strategies that apply NER or LLM preprocessing prior to DP, and assess performance in terms of privacy leakage and extrinsic evaluation (entity and relation classification). We show that DP mechanisms alone degrade utility substantially, but combining them with linguistic preprocessing, especially LLM-based redaction, significantly improves the privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [136] [A Stackelberg Model for Hybridization in Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21436)
*Willie Kouam,Stefan Rass,Zahra Seyedi,Shahzad Ahmad,Eckhard Pfluegel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从博弈论视角研究密码学中的混合加密策略，将问题建模为资源约束下的Stackelberg博弈：防御者随机选择加密算法（经典、后量子或混合），攻击者观察到选择后优化其密码分析方法。攻击者子问题用动态规划求解，防御者的Stackelberg优化则转化为线性规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将密码学中的混合加密问题形式化为一个Stackelberg博弈模型，在资源约束下同时考虑防御者（选择加密算法以平衡安全性与成本）和攻击者（选择密码分析方法以破解算法）的理性互动。

Method: 1. 将防御者与攻击者的交互建模为Stackelberg博弈；2. 攻击者观察到防御者选择的加密算法后，求解条件优化问题（"攻击者子博弈"），采用动态规划方法；3. 防御者的Stackelberg优化问题则转化为线性规划求解。

Result: 提出了一个可计算的博弈论框架：攻击者子问题可通过动态规划高效求解，防御者的最优混合策略可通过线性规划获得。

Conclusion: 该Stackelberg博弈模型为资源约束下的密码混合加密策略选择提供了理论框架和计算方法，能够帮助防御者在安全性和成本之间做出最优权衡。

Abstract: Similar to a strategic interaction between rational and intelligent agents, cryptography problems can be examined through the prism of game theory. In this setting, the agent aiming to protect a message is called the defender, while the one attempting to decrypt it, generally for malicious purposes, is the attacker. To strengthen security in cryptography, various strategies have been developed, among which hybridization stands out as a key concept in modern cryptographic design. This strategy allows the defender to select among different encryption algorithms (classical, post-quantum, or hybrid) while carefully balancing security and operational costs. On the other side, the attacker, limited by available resources, chooses cryptanalysis methods capable of breaching the selected algorithm. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg cryptographic hybridization problem under resource constraints. Here, the defender randomizes over encryption algorithms, and the attacker observes the choice before selecting suitable cryptanalysis methods. The attacker's decision is framed as a conditional optimization problem, which we refer to as the ``attacker subgame''. We then propose a dynamic programming approach for the attacker's subgame, while the defender's Stackelberg optimization is formulated as a linear program.

</details>


### [137] [MCP Pitfall Lab: Exposing Developer Pitfalls in MCP Tool Server Security under Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21477)
*Run Hao,Zhuoran Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MCP Pitfall Lab，一个面向MCP协议的协议感知安全测试框架，通过可复现的开发者陷阱场景、MCP追踪验证和客观评估器，系统性地评估和加固MCP工具服务器在多种攻击向量下的安全性。实验表明，静态分析可在四类可静态检查的漏洞类别上达到F1=1.0，而推荐加固措施可在平均仅27行代码修改下消除所有Tier-1发现并将风险评分降至0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP（模型上下文协议）在工具集成LLM智能体中日益普及，但其多层架构和第三方服务器生态扩大了工具元数据、不可信输出、跨工具流、多模态输入和供应链向量等多维风险。现有MCP基准主要衡量对恶意输入的鲁棒性，但提供的修复指导有限，缺乏系统化的安全测试和加固方法。

Method: 提出MCP Pitfall Lab框架：1) 将开发者陷阱操作化为可复现的安全场景；2) 使用MCP追踪和客观验证器（而非智能体自述）验证结果；3) 实例化三个工作流挑战（邮件、文档、加密）及六个服务器变体（基准和加固版）；4) 建模三类攻击家族（工具元数据投毒、傀儡服务器、多模态图像到工具链）；5) 设计分层评估体系。

Result: Tier-1静态分析在六种变体上的36个二分类标签中，四类可静态检查的陷阱类别（P1, P2, P5, P6）达到F1=1.0；跨工具转发和图像到工具泄露（P3, P4）被识别为依赖追踪/数据流。应用加固后所有Tier-1发现从29降至0，框架风险评分从10.0降至0.0，平均仅需修改27行代码。在19次邮件系统挑战运行中，智能体叙述与追踪证据在63.2%的运行中不一致，在sink-action运行中达100%。

Conclusion: MCP Pitfall Lab能够实现MCP工具服务器在实际多向量条件下的端到端评估与加固，追踪驱动的审计和回归测试对于保障MCP生态系统安全至关重要。

Abstract: Model Context Protocol (MCP) is increasingly adopted for tool-integrated LLM agents, but its multi-layer design and third-party server ecosystem expand risks across tool metadata, untrusted outputs, cross-tool flows, multimodal inputs, and supply-chain vectors. Existing MCP benchmarks largely measure robustness to malicious inputs but offer limited remediation guidance. We present MCP Pitfall Lab, a protocol-aware security testing framework that operationalizes developer pitfalls as reproducible scenarios and validates outcomes with MCP traces and objective validators (rather than agent self-report). We instantiate three workflow challenges (email, document, crypto) with six server variants (baseline and hardened) and model three attack families: tool-metadata poisoning, puppet servers, and multimodal image-to-tool chains, in a unified, trace-grounded evaluation. In Tier-1 static analysis over six variants (36 binary labels), our analyzer achieves F1 = 1.0 on four statically checkable pitfall classes (P1, P2, P5, P6) and flags cross-tool forwarding and image-to-tool leakage (P3, P4) as trace/dataflow-dependent. Applying recommended hardening eliminates all Tier-1 findings (29 to 0) and reduces the framework risk score (10.0 to 0.0) at a mean cost of 27 lines of code (LOC). Finally, in a preliminary 19-run corpus from the email system challenge (tool poisoning and puppet attacks), agent narratives diverge from trace evidence in 63.2% of runs and 100% of sink-action runs, motivating trace-based auditing and regression testing. Overall, Pitfall Lab enables practical, end-to-end assessment and hardening of MCP tool servers under realistic multi-vector conditions.

</details>


### [138] [Benchmarking the Utility of Privacy-Preserving Cox Regression Under Data-Driven Clipping Bounds: A Multi-Dataset Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21491)
*Keita Fukuyama,Yukiko Mori,Tomohiro Kuroda,Hiroaki Kikuchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了差分隐私（DP）机制（Laplace机制和随机响应）在Cox比例风险模型中的统计效用影响。使用5个临床数据集（n=168~6,524）、15个ε水平（0.1~1000）和1,000次蒙特卡洛迭代。主要发现：在标准DP水平（ε≤1）下，约90%的显著协变量丧失显著性，预测性能接近随机水平；输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性）在ε≥5时表现最佳；实际应用中ε需达到10（预测性能）至30~60（显著性保持）才能恢复可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私虽能保证个体隐私，但其对生存分析中统计效用的系统性影响尚未被充分评估。现有研究缺乏对多种DP机制在不同数据集规模、隐私预算下的全面比较，尤其是在Cox比例风险模型这一广泛使用的生存分析工具中。

Method: 使用数据驱动裁剪边界（观测最小/最大值，不提供正式ε-DP保证）的Laplace机制和随机响应，比较三类输入扰动（仅协变量、全部输入、离散时间模型）与输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性）。评估指标包括显著性丧失率（LSR）、C-index和系数偏倚。

Result: ε≤1时，90-94%的显著协变量丧失显著性，C-index接近0.5；仅扰动协变量的输入扰动方法保留了风险集结构且恢复最佳；输出扰动在ε≥5时接近基线性能；n≈3,000时显著性在ε=3-10间快速恢复；实践中需ε≥10（预测性能）至ε≥30-60（显著性保持）；中等ε下假阳性率增加。

Conclusion: 差分隐私对生存分析的统计效用有显著影响，尤其在低ε区域。不同DP机制和扰动策略在效用-隐私权衡上表现各异，输出扰动在高ε下更优，而仅扰动协变量的输入扰动在中等ε下恢复更快。实际部署需根据数据规模和任务目标谨慎选择ε水平。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is a mathematical framework that guarantees individual privacy; however, systematic evaluation of its impact on statistical utility in survival analyses remains limited. In this study, we systematically evaluated the impact of DP mechanisms (Laplace mechanism and Randomized Response) with data-driven clipping bounds on the Cox proportional hazards model, using 5 clinical datasets ($n = 168$--$6{,}524$), 15 levels of $\varepsilon$ (0.1--1000), and $B = 1{,}000$ Monte Carlo iterations. The data-driven clipping bounds used here are observed min/max and therefore do not provide formal $\varepsilon$-DP guarantees; the results represent an optimistic lower bound on utility degradation under formal DP. We compared three types of input perturbations (covariates only, all inputs, and the discrete-time model) with output perturbations (dfbeta-based sensitivity), using loss of significance rate (LSR), C-index, and coefficient bias as metrics. At standard DP levels ($\varepsilon \leq 1$), approximately 90% (90--94%) of the significant covariates lost significance, even in the largest dataset ($n = 6{,}524$), and the predictive performance approached random levels (test C-index $\approx 0.5$) under many conditions. Among the input perturbation approaches, perturbing only covariates preserved the risk-set structure and achieved the best recovery, whereas output perturbation (dfbeta-based sensitivity) maintained near-baseline performance at $\varepsilon \geq 5$. At $n \approx 3{,}000$, the significance recovered rapidly at $\varepsilon = 3$--10; however, in practice, $\varepsilon \geq 10$ (for predictive performance) to $\varepsilon \geq 30$--60 (for significance preservation) is required. In the moderate-to-high $\varepsilon$ range, false-positive rates increased for variables whose baseline $p$-values were near the significance threshold.

</details>


### [139] [Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against LLMs Based on Natural Style Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21700)
*Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Guoheng Sun,Xicheng Zhang,Haijun Wang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadStyle是一个完整的后门攻击框架，利用LLM生成带有不可感知风格级触发器的自然毒化样本，通过辅助目标损失稳定payload注入，在多种受害者LLM上实现高攻击成功率并保持强隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM的后门攻击方法存在三个关键缺陷：显式触发模式损害自然性、长文本生成中攻击者指定payload注入不可靠、威胁模型不完整导致后门传递和激活方式不清晰。

Method: BadStyle框架利用LLM作为毒化样本生成器，构造携带不可察觉风格级触发器的自然毒化样本（保持语义和流畅性）；设计辅助目标损失函数，在微调过程中强化毒化输入中的目标内容并惩罚其在良性响应中出现；基于现实威胁模型，在prompt诱导和PEFT两种注入策略下系统评估。

Result: 在LLaMA、Phi、DeepSeek、GPT系列等7个受害者LLM上的实验表明，BadStyle实现高攻击成功率和强隐蔽性；辅助目标损失平均提升约30%的ASR；后门在未见过的下游部署场景中仍然有效；能一致规避代表性输入级防御并通过简单伪装绕过输出级防御。

Conclusion: BadStyle通过风格级触发器和辅助目标损失，有效解决了现有后门攻击的自然性、稳定性和威胁模型完整性问题，为LLM安全性研究提供了新的视角和挑战。

Abstract: The growing application of large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical domains has raised urgent concerns about their security. Many recent studies have demonstrated the feasibility of backdoor attacks against LLMs. However, existing methods suffer from three key shortcomings: explicit trigger patterns that compromise naturalness, unreliable injection of attacker-specified payloads in long-form generation, and incompletely specified threat models that obscure how backdoors are delivered and activated in practice. To address these gaps, we present BadStyle, a complete backdoor attack framework and pipeline. BadStyle leverages an LLM as a poisoned sample generator to construct natural and stealthy poisoned samples that carry imperceptible style-level triggers while preserving semantics and fluency. To stabilize payload injection during fine-tuning, we design an auxiliary target loss that reinforces the attacker-specified target content in responses to poisoned inputs and penalizes its emergence in benign responses. We further ground the attack in a realistic threat model and systematically evaluate BadStyle under both prompt-induced and PEFT-based injection strategies. Extensive experiments across seven victim LLMs, including LLaMA, Phi, DeepSeek, and GPT series, demonstrate that BadStyle achieves high attack success rates (ASRs) while maintaining strong stealthiness. The proposed auxiliary target loss substantially improves the stability of backdoor activation, yielding an average ASR improvement of around 30% across style-level triggers. Even in downstream deployment scenarios unknown during injection, the implanted backdoor remains effective. Moreover, BadStyle consistently evades representative input-level defenses and bypasses output-level defenses through simple camouflage.

</details>


### [140] [Adversarial Robustness of Near-Field Millimeter-Wave Imaging under Waveform-Domain Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21774)
*Lhamo Dorje,Jordan Madden,Soamar Homsi,Xiaohua Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了毫米波成像在波形域物理攻击下的对抗鲁棒性，提出了一种可微成像攻击框架，实验表明毫米波成像极易受此类攻击影响，且深度学习算法比传统算法更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场毫米波成像已广泛应用于机场安检等安全关键场景，但其自身的安全性尚未得到充分研究，存在被恶意攻击的风险。

Method: 提出了一种实用白盒对抗模型和可微成像攻击框架，通过优化攻击波形来操纵图像重建过程，并构建了真实毫米波成像测试平台的实测数据集。

Result: 在10种代表性成像算法上的实验表明，毫米波成像高度脆弱，攻击者能以中等发射功率隐藏或篡改目标；深度学习算法的鲁棒性优于传统算法。

Conclusion: 这些发现暴露了关键的安全风险，亟需开发鲁棒且安全的毫米波成像系统。

Abstract: Near-field millimeter-wave (mmWave) imaging is widely deployed in safety-critical applications such as airport passenger screening, yet its own security remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic study of the adversarial robustness of mmWave imaging algorithms under waveform-domain physical attacks that directly manipulate the image reconstruction process. We propose a practical white-box adversarial model and develop a differential imaging attack framework that leverages the differentiable imaging pipeline to optimize attack waveforms. We also construct a real measured dataset of clean and attack waveforms using a mmWave imaging testbed. Experiments on 10 representative imaging algorithms show that mmWave imaging is highly vulnerable to such attacks, enabling an adversary to conceal or alter targets with moderate transmission power. Surprisingly, deep-learning-based imaging algorithms demonstrate higher robustness than classical algorithms. These findings expose critical security risks and motivate the development of robust and secure mmWave imaging systems.

</details>


### [141] [Black-Box Skill Stealing Attack from Proprietary LLM Agents: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21829)
*Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Yu Liu,Chi Liu,Qingchuan Zhao,Hongwei Li,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究针对LLM Agent系统的黑盒技能窃取攻击，发现仅需3次交互即可提取受版权保护的技能内容，并探讨了相应防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM Agent技能经济兴起（免费市场已有90368个技能，付费市场超2000个技能列表和10万美元创作者收入），技能作为封装专家知识的可复用能力面临被敌对者通过公共Agent交互窃取的风险，但目前缺乏对此攻击面的系统性研究。

Method: 基于先前的提示窃取方法推导出攻击分类法，构建自动化窃取提示生成Agent，通过模型生成的种子提示出发，结合场景合理化与结构注入进行扩展，并利用嵌入过滤保证多样性，形成可复现的评估流水线。

Result: 在3种商业Agent架构和5种LLM上的评估表明，仅需3次交互即可提取Agent技能，构成严重版权风险；提出的三阶段（输入、推理、输出）防御虽有效，但攻击代价低且可自动化重复尝试，只需一次成功即可攻破。

Conclusion: 专有Agent生态系统普遍忽视了技能版权风险，亟需更强有力的防御策略提供更可靠的保护保障。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on skills to encapsulate reusable capabilities via progressively disclosed instructions. High-quality skills inject expert knowledge into general-purpose models, improving performance on specialized tasks. This quality and ease of dissemination drive the emergence of a skill economy: free skill marketplaces already report 90368 published skills, while paid marketplaces report more than 2000 listings and over $100,000 in creator earnings. Yet this growing marketplace also creates a new attack surface, as adversaries can interact with public agent to extract hidden proprietary skill content. We present the first empirical study of black-box skill stealing against LLM agent systems. To study this threat, we first derive an attack taxonomy from prior prompt-stealing methods and build an automated stealing prompt generation agent. This agent starts from model-generated seed prompts, expands them through scenario rationalization and structure injection, and enforces diversity via embedding filtering. This process yields a reproducible pipeline for evaluating agent systems. We evaluate such attacks across 3 commercial agent architectures and 5 LLMs. Our results show that agent skills can be extracted with only 3 interactions, posing a serious copyright risk. To mitigate this threat, we design defenses across three stages of the agent pipeline: input, inference, and output. Although these defenses achieve strong results, the attack remains inexpensive and readily automatable, allowing an adversary to launch repeated attempts with different variants; only one successful attempt is sufficient to compromise the protected skill. Overall, our findings suggest that these copyright risks are largely overlooked across proprietary agent ecosystems. We therefore advocate for more robust defense strategies that provide stronger protection guarantees.

</details>


### [142] [Cross-Modal Phantom: Coordinated Camera-LiDAR Spoofing Against Multi-Sensor Fusion in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21841)
*Shahriar Rahman Khan,Raiful Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现自动驾驶多传感器融合(MSF)存在一种新型漏洞：攻击者可以伪造跨传感器一致性，使相机和LiDAR同时感知到相同的虚假目标。通过协调的红外投影和LiDAR信号注入模拟攻击，在KITTI数据集上达到85.5%的攻击成功率，首次定量证明了恶意跨模态一致性可以破坏MSF感知系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器融合(MSF)被设计用于通过冗余来抵御单传感器故障，但融合过程本身可能引入新的、未被充分探索的脆弱性。作者旨在研究攻击者是否能够通过制造跨传感器一致性来绕过MSF的冗余保护机制。

Method: 设计了协调的、数据级（早期融合）攻击：模拟两个同步物理欺骗源（红外投影和LiDAR信号注入）的传感器输出结果，通过插入透视感知的图像补丁和3D空间中对齐的合成LiDAR点簇来模拟攻击效果，而非实现实际物理攻击硬件。

Result: 在400个KITTI场景的大规模评估中，协调欺骗攻击对最先进的感知模型达到了85.5%的成功攻击率。

Conclusion: 首次提供定量证据表明恶意跨模态一致性可以破坏基于MSF的感知系统，揭示了现代自动驾驶系统核心数据融合逻辑中的关键漏洞。

Abstract: Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) increasingly depend on Multi-Sensor Fusion (MSF) to combine complementary modalities such as cameras and LiDAR for robust perception. While this redundancy is intended to safeguard against single-sensor failures, the fusion process itself introduces a subtle and underexplored vulnerability. In this work, we investigate whether an attacker can bypass MSF's redundancy by fabricating cross-sensor consistency, making multiple sensors agree on the same false object. We design a coordinated, data-level (early-fusion) attack that emulates the outcome of two synchronized physical spoofing sources: an infrared (IR) projection that induces a false camera detection and a LiDAR signal injection that produces a matching 3D point cluster. Rather than implementing the physical attack hardware, we simulate its sensor-level outcomes by inserting perspective-aware image patches and synthetic LiDAR point clusters aligned in 3D space. This approach preserves the perceptual effects that real IR and IEMI-based spoofing would create at the sensor output. Using 400 KITTI scenes, our large-scale evaluation shows that the coordinated spoofing deceives a state-of-the-art perception model with an 85.5% successful attack rate.
