These findings provide the first quantitative evidence that malicious cross-modal consistency can compromise MSF-based perception, revealing a critical vulnerability in the core data-fusion logic of modern autonomous vehicle systems.

</details>


### [143] [CrossCommitVuln-Bench: A Dataset of Multi-Commit Python Vulnerabilities Invisible to Per-Commit Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21917)
*Arunabh Majumdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CrossCommitVuln-Bench基准，包含15个真实Python漏洞，其可利用条件跨多个提交引入。评估表明：每次提交的静态分析检测率仅13%，累计模式也仅27%，证明跨提交漏洞对现有SAST工具具有高度隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAST工具主要以单次提交或快照为分析单位，无法检测跨越多个提交、单个提交看似无害但组合后形成漏洞的缺陷。作者旨在通过构建跨提交漏洞基准，揭示这一检测盲区并推动相关研究。

Method: 人工筛选并标注15个真实Python CVE漏洞，标注每个CVE的贡献提交链、每个提交规避单次分析的结构化理由；使用Semgrep和Bandit在逐提交扫描和累积扫描两种模式下进行基线评估。

Result: 逐提交检测率(CCDR)仅为13%（87%的漏洞链对逐提交SAST不可见），且检测到的案例质量差：一次是对安全修复提交的误报（开发者忽略告警），另一次仅检测到次要硬编码密钥问题而完全遗漏主要漏洞。累积模式检测率也仅27%。

Conclusion: 现有基于快照的SAST工具普遍难以检测跨多个提交引入的漏洞，需要研究专门针对跨提交漏洞检测的新方法。数据集、标注方案和评估脚本均已开源。

Abstract: We present CrossCommitVuln-Bench, a curated benchmark of 15 real-world Python vulnerabilities (CVEs) in which the exploitable condition was introduced across multiple commits - each individually benign to per-commit static analysis - but collectively critical. We manually annotate each CVE with its contributing commit chain, a structured rationale for why each commit evades per-commit analysis, and baseline evaluations using Semgrep and Bandit in both per-commit and cumulative scanning modes. Our central finding: the per-commit detection rate (CCDR) is 13% across all 15 vulnerabilities - 87% of chains are invisible to per-commit SAST. Critically, both per-commit detections are qualitatively poor: one occurs on commits framed as security fixes (where developers suppress the alert), and the other detects only the minor hardcoded-key component while completely missing the primary vulnerability (200+ unprotected API endpoints). Even in cumulative mode (full codebase present), the detection rate is only 27%, confirming that snapshot-based SAST tools often miss vulnerabilities whose introduction spans multiple commits. The dataset, annotation schema, evaluation scripts, and reproducible baselines are released under open-source licenses to support research on cross-commit vulnerability detection.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Achieving the Kesten-Stigum bound in the non-uniform hypergraph stochastic block model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20907)
*Manuel Fernandez,Ludovic Stephan,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了非均匀超图随机块模型中的社区检测问题，建立了弱恢复的Kesten-Stigum型界，证明当所有均匀超图层信噪比之和超过1时弱恢复可行，并提出了基于最优加权非回溯算子的多项式时间谱算法，同时发展了加权非回溯算子的谱理论及Ihara-Bass公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均匀超图中存在多种大小的超边，这引出了核心问题：能否将多个低于检测阈值的均匀超图层组合起来实现弱恢复？

Method: 建立了非均匀HSBM中弱恢复的Kesten-Stigum型界；提出了基于最优加权非回溯算子的多项式时间谱算法；发展了加权非回溯算子的谱理论，包括异常特征值和特征向量重叠的精确刻画；引入了适用于加权非均匀超图的Ihara-Bass公式。

Result: 对r=2情形，证明当所有均匀超图层信噪比之和超过1时弱恢复可行，证实了Chodrow等人(2023)猜想中的正向部分；未加权非回溯矩阵的谱方法达到了另一算法阈值，同样验证了该猜想的另一部分。

Conclusion: 这些结果为非均匀超图聚类提供了原理性且计算高效的方法，突显了最优权重在聚合异构高阶交互中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the community detection problem in the non-uniform hypergraph stochastic block model (HSBM), where hyperedges of varying sizes coexist. This setting captures higher-order and multi-view interactions and raises a fundamental question: can multiple uniform hypergraph layers below the detection threshold be combined to enable weak recovery? We answer this question by establishing a Kesten--Stigum-type bound for weak recovery in a general class of non-uniform HSBMs with $r$ blocks, generated according to multiple symmetric probability tensors. In the case $r=2$, we show that weak recovery is possible whenever the sum of the signal-to-noise ratios across all uniform hypergraph layers exceeds one, thereby confirming the positive part of a conjecture in (Chodrow et al., 2023). Moreover, we provide a polynomial-time spectral algorithm that achieves this threshold via an optimally weighted non-backtracking operator. For the unweighted non-backtracking matrix, our spectral method attains a different algorithmic threshold, also conjectured in (Chodrow et al., 2023).
  Our approach develops a spectral theory for weighted non-backtracking operators on non-uniform hypergraphs, including a precise characterization of outlier eigenvalues and eigenvector overlaps. We introduce a novel Ihara--Bass formula tailored to weighted non-uniform hypergraphs, which yields an efficient low-dimensional representation and leads to a provable spectral reconstruction algorithm. Taken together, these results provide a principled and computationally efficient approach to clustering in non-uniform hypergraphs, and highlight the role of optimal weighting in aggregating heterogeneous higher-order interactions.

</details>


### [145] [Learning to Emulate Chaos: Adversarial Optimal Transport Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21097)
*Gabriel Melo,Leonardo Santiago,Peter Y. Lu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于对抗最优传输的框架，通过Sinkhorn散度（2-Wasserstein）和WGAN对偶形式（1-Wasserstein）联合学习高质量摘要统计量和物理一致的混沌系统仿真器，在多种混沌系统中显著提升长期统计保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌系统对初始条件敏感，传统基于平方误差的损失在噪声数据上训练时表现不佳，难以实现精确长期预测；现有方法依赖手工设计的局部特征和摘要统计量作为正则化，缺乏自动学习高质量统计量的能力。

Method: 提出一类对抗最优传输目标函数，联合学习摘要统计量和物理一致的仿真器；理论分析并实验验证了两种形式：基于Sinkhorn散度的2-Wasserstein距离和基于WGAN对偶形式的1-Wasserstein距离。

Result: 在多种混沌系统（包括高维混沌吸引子系统）上的实验表明，使用该方法训练的仿真器在长期统计保真度上显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 对抗最优传输方法能有效训练混沌系统的数据驱动仿真器，在不依赖手工特征的情况下实现长期统计匹配，为混沌动力系统的仿真建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Chaos arises in many complex dynamical systems, from weather to power grids, but is difficult to accurately model using data-driven emulators, including neural operator architectures. For chaotic systems, the inherent sensitivity to initial conditions makes exact long-term forecasts theoretically infeasible, meaning that traditional squared-error losses often fail when trained on noisy data. Recent work has focused on training emulators to match the statistical properties of chaotic attractors by introducing regularization based on handcrafted local features and summary statistics, as well as learned statistics extracted from a diverse dataset of trajectories. In this work, we propose a family of adversarial optimal transport objectives that jointly learn high-quality summary statistics and a physically consistent emulator. We theoretically analyze and experimentally validate a Sinkhorn divergence formulation (2-Wasserstein) and a WGAN-style dual formulation (1-Wasserstein). Our experiments across a variety of chaotic systems, including systems with high-dimensional chaotic attractors, show that emulators trained with our approach exhibit significantly improved long-term statistical fidelity.

</details>


### [146] [Refining Covariance Matrix Estimation in Stochastic Gradient Descent Through Bias Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21203)
*Ziyang Wei,Wanrong Zhu,Jingyang Lyu,Wei Biao Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对随机梯度下降（SGD）算法，提出了一种新颖的全在线无偏协方差估计器，无需二阶导数（Hessian矩阵）信息，通过偏差缩减技术实现了$n^{(α-1)/2} \sqrt{\log n}$的收敛速度，优于现有免Hessian方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGD在线推断方法存在两大问题：经典方法（如插件法、批均值法）要么需要难以获取的Hessian信息，要么收敛速度慢。为解决这些挑战，需要一种无需二阶导数且具有更高估计精度的在线协方差估计方法。

Method: 提出一种全在线去偏协方差估计器，采用偏差缩减技术，完全消除了对二阶导数（Hessian矩阵）的需求，同时显著提升了估计精度。

Result: 该方法的收敛速度达到$n^{(α-1)/2} \sqrt{\log n}$，优于现有的免Hessian替代方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的全在线去偏协方差估计器在无需二阶导数信息的前提下，实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的估计精度，为SGD在线推断提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We study online inference and asymptotic covariance estimation for the stochastic gradient descent (SGD) algorithm. While classical methods (such as plug-in and batch-means estimators) are available, they either require inaccessible second-order (Hessian) information or suffer from slow convergence. To address these challenges, we propose a novel, fully online de-biased covariance estimator that eliminates the need for second-order derivatives while significantly improving estimation accuracy. Our method employs a bias-reduction technique to achieve a convergence rate of $n^{(α-1)/2} \sqrt{\log n}$, outperforming existing Hessian-free alternatives.

</details>


### [147] [Calibeating Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21260)
*Lars van der Laan,Mark Van Der Laan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出校准预测驱动推断（Calibrated PPI），通过对黑箱预测模型的输出进行事后校准（线性或等渗校准），提升半监督均值估计的效率和预测准确性，并建立了等渗校准的一阶最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半监督均值估计中，标准方法AIPW虽能防护预测模型误设，但当预测分数与结果尺度对齐不良时效率低下。本文旨在通过简单的校准步骤提升效率，无需重新训练模型。

Method: 在标记样本上对黑箱预测模型输出进行事后校准（线性校准和等渗校准），再将校准后的分数用于半监督估计。理论分析上，建立了等渗校准的一阶最优性保证，并澄清了PPI、PPI++、AIPW之间的关系。

Result: 等渗校准可提升预测精度和估计效率，且进一步后处理无额外一阶收益；线性校准与PPI++一阶等价。实验表明校准估计器通常优于PPI，与AIPW和PPI++相当或更优。

Conclusion: 事后校准是一种简单有效的半监督估计改进策略，理论上有严格保证，实践中有显著收益。

Abstract: We study semisupervised mean estimation with a small labeled sample, a large unlabeled sample, and a black-box prediction model whose output may be miscalibrated. A standard approach in this setting is augmented inverse-probability weighting (AIPW) [Robins et al., 1994], which protects against prediction-model misspecification but can be inefficient when the prediction score is poorly aligned with the outcome scale. We introduce Calibrated Prediction-Powered Inference, which post-hoc calibrates the prediction score on the labeled sample before using it for semisupervised estimation. This simple step requires no retraining and can improve the original score both as a predictor of the outcome and as a regression adjustment for semisupervised inference. We study both linear and isotonic calibration. For isotonic calibration, we establish first-order optimality guarantees: isotonic post-processing can improve predictive accuracy and estimator efficiency relative to the original score and simpler post-processing rules, while no further post-processing of the fitted isotonic score yields additional first-order gains. For linear calibration, we show first-order equivalence to PPI++. We also clarify the relationship among existing estimators, showing that the original PPI estimator is a special case of AIPW and can be inefficient when the prediction model is accurate, while PPI++ is AIPW with empirical efficiency maximization [Rubin et al., 2008]. In simulations and real-data experiments, our calibrated estimators often outperform PPI and are competitive with, or outperform, AIPW and PPI++. We provide an accompanying Python package, ppi_aipw, at https://larsvanderlaan.github.io/ppi-aipw/.

</details>


### [148] [CLT-Optimal Parameter Error Bounds for Linear System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21270)
*Yichen Zhou,Stephen Tu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有线性动力系统（LDS）参数估计的有限样本边界显著高估了实际统计复杂度（误差被高估了状态维度倍），并提出了一个基于二阶分解的改进方法，在Frobenius范数和谱范数上分别达到了实例最优率（常数因子内）和多对数状态维度因子内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于线性动力系统（LDS）通过普通最小二乘（OLS）进行系统辨识的有限样本非渐近界并不准确，无法真实反映系统识别的统计复杂度。具体来说，现有界在谱范数和Frobenius范数下将参数平方误差高估了状态维度的倍数，亟需纠正这种偏差。

Method: 利用渐近正态性识别出现有界高估的问题实例类别；然后通过一种新颖的二阶参数误差分解方法——其中低阶项是一个矩阵值鞅，能够正确捕捉中心极限定理（CLT）的缩放——来锐化OLS参数误差界。

Result: 得到了两种情形下的有限样本界：（i）稳定系统 和（ii）多轨迹设定。在Frobenius范数下与实例最优率匹配到常数因子；在谱范数下匹配到多对数状态维度因子。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶分解方法能够显著缩小理论与实际统计复杂度之间的差距，为系统辨识提供了更紧且更准确的有限样本保证。

Abstract: There has been remarkable progress over the past decade in establishing finite-sample, non-asymptotic bounds on recovering unknown system parameters from observed system behavior. Surprisingly, however, we show that the current state-of-the-art bounds do not accurately capture the statistical complexity of system identification, even in the most fundamental setting of estimating a discrete-time linear dynamical system (LDS) via ordinary least-squares regression (OLS). Specifically, we utilize asymptotic normality to identify classes of problem instances for which current bounds overstate the squared parameter error, in both spectral and Frobenius norm, by a factor of the state-dimension of the system. Informed by this discrepancy, we then sharpen the OLS parameter error bounds via a novel second-order decomposition of the parameter error, where crucially the lower-order term is a matrix-valued martingale that we show correctly captures the CLT scaling. From our analysis we obtain finite-sample bounds for both (i) stable systems and (ii) the many-trajectories setting that match the instance-specific optimal rates up to constant factors in Frobenius norm, and polylogarithmic state-dimension factors in spectral norm.

</details>


### [149] [A single algorithm for both restless and rested rotting bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21432)
*Julien Seznec,Pierre Ménard,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RAW-UCB算法，在无需先验知识的情况下，在腐烂的rested和restless bandit设置中均实现了接近最优的遗憾值，挑战了先前认为这两种设置存在本质差异的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用（如推荐系统、智能辅导系统）中，动作的奖励会随时间衰减，这种衰减可能由过去执行的动作引起（如用户对重复推荐感到厌倦）或由外部因素导致（如内容过时）。先前认为rested和restless bandit这两种腐烂设置存在显著差异，且缺乏能够同时处理两者的有效算法。

Method: 提出Rotting Adaptive Window UCB (RAW-UCB)算法，该算法能够自适应地调整窗口大小，无需预先知道设置类型（rested或restless）以及非平稳性类型（如分段常数、有界变差）。

Result: RAW-UCB在腐烂的rested和restless bandit两种设置下均实现了接近最优的遗憾值，这与先前认为奖励允许增加时没有算法能取得类似结果的负面结论形成鲜明对比。在合成数据和基于真实数据集上的实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: RAW-UCB成功统一了腐烂的rested和restless bandit问题，证明了在奖励仅下降（腐烂）的情况下，可以设计出同时适用于两种设置的近最优算法，突破了先前认为不可能的局限。

Abstract: In many application domains (e.g., recommender systems, intelligent tutoring systems), the rewards associated to the actions tend to decrease over time. This decay is either caused by the actions executed in the past (e.g., a user may get bored when songs of the same genre are recommended over and over) or by an external factor (e.g., content becomes outdated). These two situations can be modeled as specific instances of the rested and restless bandit settings, where arms are rotting (i.e., their value decrease over time). These problems were thought to be significantly different, since Levine et al. (2017) showed that state-of-the-art algorithms for restless bandit perform poorly in the rested rotting setting. In this paper, we introduce a novel algorithm, Rotting Adaptive Window UCB (RAW-UCB), that achieves near-optimal regret in both rotting rested and restless bandit, without any prior knowledge of the setting (rested or restless) and the type of non-stationarity (e.g., piece-wise constant, bounded variation). This is in striking contrast with previous negative results showing that no algorithm can achieve similar results as soon as rewards are allowed to increase. We confirm our theoretical findings on a number of synthetic and dataset-based experiments.

</details>


### [150] [A Kernel Nonconformity Score for Multivariate Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21595)
*Louis Meyer,Wenkai Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出多变量核分数（MKS），用于多变量共形预测，通过核方法生成自适应预测区域，在保证覆盖率的条件下显著减小预测区域体积，并实现维度无关的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量共形预测需要将残差向量压缩为标量的非一致性分数，同时保留残差分布的隐式几何结构，现有方法未能充分适应这种几何结构。

Method: 提出多变量核分数（MKS），其形式类似于高斯过程后验方差，可分解为各向异性的最大均值差异（MMD），介于核密度估计和协方差加权距离之间。

Result: 证明了有限样本覆盖保证，建立了依赖于核协方差算子有效秩（而非环境维度）的收敛速率；在回归任务中，相比椭球基线方法显著减小预测区域体积，维度越高、覆盖水平越严格时收益越大。

Conclusion: MKS统一了贝叶斯不确定性量化与频率学派覆盖保证，实现了维度无关的自适应预测区域构建。

Abstract: Multivariate conformal prediction requires nonconformity scores that compress residual vectors into scalars while preserving certain implicit geometric structure of the residual distribution. We introduce a Multivariate Kernel Score (MKS) that produces prediction regions that explicitly adapt to this geometry. We show that the proposed score resembles the Gaussian process posterior variance, unifying Bayesian uncertainty quantification with the coverage guarantees of frequentist-type. Moreover, the MKS can be decomposed into an anisotropic Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) that interpolates between kernel density estimation and covariance-weighted distance. We prove finite-sample coverage guarantees and establish convergence rates that depend on the effective rank of the kernel-based covariance operator rather than the ambient dimension, enabling dimension-free adaptation. On regression tasks, the MKS reduces the volume of prediction region significantly, compared to ellipsoidal baselines while maintaining nominal coverage, with larger gains at higher dimensions and tighter coverage levels.

</details>


### [151] [There Will Be a Scientific Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21691)
*Jamie Simon,Daniel Kunin,Alexander Atanasov,Enric Boix-Adserà,Blake Bordelon,Jeremy Cohen,Nikhil Ghosh,Florentin Guth,Arthur Jacot,Mason Kamb,Dhruva Karkada,Eric J. Michaud,Berkan Ottlik,Joseph Turnbull*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出深度学习科学理论正在形成，称为"学习力学"，它关注训练过程的动力学、粗粒度聚合统计量和可证伪的定量预测，由五个研究方向构成：可解理想化设定、可处理极限、简单数学定律、超参数理论、普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证深度学习正在形成一种科学理论，并将其系统化地归纳为"学习力学"框架，回应"深度学习不可能有基础理论"或"理论不重要"的质疑。

Method: 综合梳理深度学习理论的五个主要研究方向：(a)可解理想化设定、(b)可处理极限、(c)简单数学定律、(d)超参数理论、(e)普适行为；并对比统计视角和信息论视角，分析其与机械可解释性的共生关系。

Result: 识别出五个共同特征：关注训练过程动力学、描述粗粒度聚合统计、强调可证伪的定量预测；提出了"学习力学"这一统一命名；指出了与统计视角、信息论视角及机械可解释性的关系。

Conclusion: 学习力学是正在形成的深度学习理论框架，未来方向包括开放问题的梳理和初学者的引导，并提供了学习力学的公共资源平台。

Abstract: In this paper, we make the case that a scientific theory of deep learning is emerging. By this we mean a theory which characterizes important properties and statistics of the training process, hidden representations, final weights, and performance of neural networks. We pull together major strands of ongoing research in deep learning theory and identify five growing bodies of work that point toward such a theory: (a) solvable idealized settings that provide intuition for learning dynamics in realistic systems; (b) tractable limits that reveal insights into fundamental learning phenomena; (c) simple mathematical laws that capture important macroscopic observables; (d) theories of hyperparameters that disentangle them from the rest of the training process, leaving simpler systems behind; and (e) universal behaviors shared across systems and settings which clarify which phenomena call for explanation.
  Taken together, these bodies of work share certain broad traits: they are concerned with the dynamics of the training process; they primarily seek to describe coarse aggregate statistics; and they emphasize falsifiable quantitative predictions. We argue that the emerging theory is best thought of as a mechanics of the learning process, and suggest the name learning mechanics. We discuss the relationship between this mechanics perspective and other approaches for building a theory of deep learning, including the statistical and information-theoretic perspectives. In particular, we anticipate a symbiotic relationship between learning mechanics and mechanistic interpretability.
  We also review and address common arguments that fundamental theory will not be possible or is not important. We conclude with a portrait of important open directions in learning mechanics and advice for beginners. We host further introductory materials, perspectives, and open questions at learningmechanics.pub.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Regime-Calibrated Demand Priors for Ride-Hailing Fleet Dispatch and Repositioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03883)
*Indar Kumar,Akanksha Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于需求体制校准的网约车调度方法，通过将历史数据分割为需求体制、匹配当前运营时段、利用校准先验驱动LP重定位和匈牙利批量匹配，在NYC和Chicago数据集上显著降低等待时间，无需训练且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车调度需要应对随时间段、季节和特殊事件大幅变化的需求模式，现有方法难以有效捕捉这种复杂动态性。

Method: (i) 将历史行程数据分割为需求体制；(ii) 通过结合Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离、Wasserstein-1距离、特征距离、方差比、事件模式相似性和时间邻近性的相似性集成，匹配当前运营时段与最相似的历史类比；(iii) 使用校准后的需求先验驱动基于LP的车队重定位策略和匈牙利匹配批量调度。

Result: 在520万趟NYC TLC行程、8种场景下，平均等待时间降低31.1%（95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]），P95等待时间降低37.6%，Gini系数从0.441改善至0.409。校准贡献16.9%降低，LP重定位额外贡献15.5%。泛化至Chicago数据集实现23.3%等待时间降低。

Conclusion: 该方法无需训练、确定性、可解释，对车队规模鲁棒，且可跨城市泛化，为网约车调度提供了一种有效的非学习型替代方案。

Abstract: Effective ride-hailing dispatch requires anticipating demand patterns that vary substantially across time-of-day, day-of-week, season, and special events. We propose a regime-calibrated approach that (i) segments historical trip data into demand regimes, (ii) matches the current operating period to the most similar historical analogues via a similarity ensemble combining Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance, Wasserstein-1 distance, feature distance, variance ratio, event pattern similarity, and temporal proximity, and (iii) uses the resulting calibrated demand prior to drive both an LP-based fleet repositioning policy and batch dispatch with Hungarian matching. In ablation, a distributional-only metric subset achieves the strongest mean-wait reduction, while the full ensemble is retained as a robustness-oriented default that preserves calendar and event context.
  Evaluated on 5.2 million NYC TLC trips across 8 diverse scenarios (winter/summer, weekday/weekend/holiday, morning/evening/night) with 5 random seeds each, our method reduces mean rider wait times by 31.1% (bootstrap 95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]; Friedman chi-squared = 80.0, p = 4.25e-18; Cohen's d = 7.5-29.9). P95 wait drops 37.6% and the Gini coefficient of wait times improves from 0.441 to 0.409. The two contributions compose multiplicatively: calibration provides 16.9% reduction relative to the replay baseline; LP repositioning adds a further 15.5%. The approach requires no training, is deterministic and explainable, generalizes to Chicago (23.3% wait reduction using the NYC-built regime library without retraining), and is robust across fleet sizes (32-47% improvement for 0.5x-2.0x fleet scaling). Code is available at https://github.com/IndarKarhana/regime-calibrated-dispatch.

</details>


### [153] [Supervised Dimensionality Reduction Revisited: Why LDA on Frozen CNN Features Deserves a Second Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03928)
*Indar Kumar,Girish Karhana,Sai Krishna Jasti,Ankit Hemant Lade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于需求状态校准的网约车调度方法，通过将历史数据分割为需求状态、使用六指标相似度集合匹配当前运营时段、并基于校准后的需求先验驱动线性规划车队重定位和匈牙利批处理匹配，在纽约5.2百万条行程数据上实现平均等待时间降低31.1%，且无需训练、确定可解释、可泛化至芝加哥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车调度需要预测随时间、日类型、季节和特殊事件变化的复杂需求模式，现有方法在动态环境下鲁棒性不足。

Method: (i) 将历史行程数据分割为需求状态；(ii) 通过六指标相似度集合（KS检验、Wasserstein-1距离、特征距离、方差比、事件模式、时间邻近性）匹配当前运营时段与最相似历史片段；(iii) 利用校准后的需求先验驱动基于线性规划的车队重定位策略和基于匈牙利匹配的批处理调度。

Result: 在纽约5.2百万次行程、8种场景下平均等待时间降低31.1%（95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]%），P95等待时间降低37.6%，基尼系数从0.441降至0.409；校准贡献16.9%降低，LP重定位额外贡献15.5%；无需训练、确定可解释，泛化至芝加哥（23.3%降低），对车队规模鲁棒（0.5-2倍规模下32-47%改善）。

Conclusion: 该需求状态校准方法有效提升了网约车调度性能，组合效应可乘性叠加，且具备良好的可解释性、泛化性和鲁棒性，为实际部署提供了可靠选择。

Abstract: Effective ride-hailing dispatch requires anticipating demand patterns that vary substantially across time-of-day, day-of-week, season, and special events. We propose a regime-calibrated approach that (i) segments historical trip data into demand regimes, (ii) matches the current operating period to the most similar historical analogues via a six-metric similarity ensemble (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, Wasserstein-1, feature distance, variance ratio, event pattern, temporal proximity), and (iii) uses the resulting calibrated demand prior to drive both an LP-based fleet repositioning policy and batch dispatch with Hungarian matching. In ablation, a distributional-only subset is strongest on mean wait, while the full ensemble is retained as a robustness-oriented default.
  Evaluated on 5.2 million NYC TLC trips across 8 diverse scenarios (winter/summer, weekday/weekend/holiday, morning/evening/night) with 5 random seeds each, our method reduces mean rider wait times by 31.1% (bootstrap 95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]%; Friedman chi-sq = 80.0, p = 4.25e-18; Cohen's d = 7.5-29.9 across scenarios). The improvement extends to the tail: P95 wait drops 37.6% and the Gini coefficient of wait times improves from 0.441 to 0.409 (7.3% relative). The two contributions compose multiplicatively and are independently validated: calibration provides 16.9% reduction; LP repositioning adds a further 15.5%. The approach requires no training, is deterministic and explainable, generalizes to Chicago (23.3% wait reduction via NYC-built regime library), and is robust across fleet sizes (32-47% improvement for 0.5-2x fleet scaling). We provide comprehensive ablation studies, formal statistical tests, and routing-fidelity validation with OSRM.

</details>


### [154] [The Sample Complexity of Multicalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21923)
*Natalie Collina,Jiuyao Lu,Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了批量设置下多校准（multicalibration）的极小极大样本复杂度。对于固定的κ>0且|G|≤ε^{-κ}，证明了样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-3})（上下界匹配，仅差多对数因子），这严格大于边际校准（marginal calibration）的Θ̃(ε^{-2})。对于κ=0，复杂度降为Θ̃(ε^{-2})，呈现尖锐阈值现象。更一般地，建立了加权L_p多校准度量在1≤p≤2时的最优样本复杂度为3/p，并将下界模板推广到可elicitable性质类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多校准是机器学习公平性和预测可靠性的重要概念，但其样本复杂度的精确刻画尚未明确。理解其样本复杂度对于设计高效算法和理解其与边际校准等概念的本质区别至关重要。

Method: 通过在线到批量（online-to-batch）归约构造随机预测器实现上界；通过信息论和极小极大下界技术建立匹配的下界，并将分析推广到加权L_p度量和更一般的可elicitable性质类。

Result: 在|G|≤ε^{-κ}（κ>0）时，多校准样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-3})，与边际校准的Θ̃(ε^{-2})分离；κ=0时存在阈值现象，复杂度降为Θ̃(ε^{-2})。加权L_p度量下最优样本复杂度为3/p。下界模板可推广到expectiles和有界密度分位数等性质。

Conclusion: 批量设置下多校准的样本复杂度为ε^{-3}量级，比边际校准更困难；批量和在线设置下多校准难度相同，而边际校准在在线设置下更困难。这一结果完善了校准类问题的样本复杂度理论。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of multicalibration in the batch setting. A learner observes $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution and must output a (possibly randomized) predictor whose population multicalibration error, measured by Expected Calibration Error (ECE), is at most $\varepsilon$ with respect to a given family of groups. For every fixed $κ> 0$, in the regime $|G|\le \varepsilon^{-κ}$, we prove that $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-3})$ samples are necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors. The lower bound holds even for randomized predictors, and the upper bound is realized by a randomized predictor obtained via an online-to-batch reduction. This separates the sample complexity of multicalibration from that of marginal calibration, which scales as $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and shows that mean-ECE multicalibration is as difficult in the batch setting as it is in the online setting, in contrast to marginal calibration which is strictly more difficult in the online setting. In contrast we observe that for $κ= 0$, the sample complexity of multicalibration remains $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ exhibiting a sharp threshold phenomenon.
  More generally, we establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to polylogarithmic factors, for a weighted $L_p$ multicalibration metric for all $1 \le p \le 2$, with optimal exponent $3/p$. We also extend the lower-bound template to a regular class of elicitable properties, and combine it with the online upper bounds of Hu et al. (2025) to obtain matching bounds for calibrating properties including expectiles and bounded-density quantiles.

</details>


### [155] [Differentially Private Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20985)
*Qichuan Yin,Manzil Zaheer,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种后处理技术（随机选择和线性组合），利用已有不同隐私/效用权衡的模型，无需额外训练即可生成满足任意目标差分隐私要求的模型，并在理论上证明了线性组合的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习的推理或部署阶段，隐私要求会因政策、法规或用户体验的变化而不断改变，现有方法需要重新训练模型来满足新的隐私需求，成本高昂。

Method: 基于一组已在相同数据集上训练好的、具有不同隐私/效用权衡的模型，提出随机选择和线性组合两种后处理技术；通过Rényi差分隐私和隐私损失分布进行隐私核算；在私有均值估计案例中，从理论上完整刻画隐私/效用结果并证明线性组合优于随机选择。

Result: 在私有均值估计案例中，理论证明了线性组合在隐私/效用权衡上优于随机选择；在合成和真实数据集上的多种模型实验中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的后处理技术能够灵活高效地满足动态变化的隐私需求，无需重新训练，其中线性组合方法在理论分析和实验验证中均表现更优。

Abstract: In machine learning applications, privacy requirements during inference or deployment time could change constantly due to varying policies, regulations, or user experience. In this work, we aim to generate a magnitude of models to satisfy any target differential privacy (DP) requirement without additional training steps, given a set of existing models trained on the same dataset with different privacy/utility tradeoffs. We propose two post processing techniques, namely random selection and linear combination, to output a final private model for any target privacy parameter. We provide privacy accounting of these approaches from the lens of R'enyi DP and privacy loss distributions for general problems. In a case study on private mean estimation, we fully characterize the privacy/utility results and theoretically establish the superiority of linear combination over random selection. Empirically, we validate our approach and analyses on several models and both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [156] [Even More Guarantees for Variational Inference in the Presence of Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21407)
*Lena Zellinger,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究变分推断中变分族与目标分布不匹配时（模型误设），在何种条件下仍能准确恢复目标分布的特征（如均值）。推导了使用前向KL散度和α散度时保证均值精确恢复的充分条件，并分析了优化失败的原因，为变分族选择和α值设定提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分推断中通常使用简单的参数化变分族逼近难以处理的密度，但这些变分族很可能不包含真实目标分布（即模型误设）。因此需要研究在误设条件下，能否以及如何从优化后的变分分布中恢复目标分布的关键特征（如均值）。

Method: 理论分析：在前向KL散度和α散度的框架下，推导了在位置-尺度变分族中，利用目标分布的对称性保证均值精确恢复的充分条件；并通过反例分析缺乏这些条件时优化失败的原因。

Result: 给出了在变分族误设情况下，使用前向KL散度和α散度时精确恢复目标均值的充分条件；展示了当这些条件不满足时优化可能无法恢复目标均值，并分析了其机制。

Conclusion: 为实践中如何在模型误设下选择变分族和α值以可靠恢复目标均值提供了理论指导。

Abstract: When approximating an intractable density via variational inference (VI) the variational family is typically chosen as a simple parametric family that very likely does not contain the target. This raises the question: Under which conditions can we recover characteristics of the target despite misspecification? In this work, we extend previous results on robust VI with location-scale families under target symmetries. We derive sufficient conditions guaranteeing exact recovery of the mean when using the forward Kullback-Leibler divergence and $α$-divergences. We further show how and why optimization can fail to recover the target mean in the absence of our sufficient conditions, providing initial guidelines on the choice of the variational family and $α$-value.

</details>


### [157] [Quotient-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21809)
*Yixian Xu,Yusong Wang,Shengjie Luo,Kaiyuan Gao,Tianyu He,Di He,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个通用的商空间扩散模型框架，并应用于具有SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成，通过减少对群作用分量的学习需求来简化学习难度，同时保证采样器能恢复目标分布，在小分子和蛋白质结构生成上优于以往的对称性处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在处理具有对称性的任务时，目标分布本质上定义在商空间上，但传统群等变扩散模型需要学习群作用对应的分量，增加了学习难度。缺乏一个通用的商空间扩散建模框架来处理这类问题。

Method: 建立了一个通用的商空间扩散模型框架，将扩散过程定义在商空间上，减少对群作用分量的学习；应用于SE(3)对称的分子结构生成，并设计了保证恢复目标分布的采样器。

Result: 在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上，商空间扩散模型优于传统的对称性处理方法，实验验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 商空间扩散模型为处理具有对称性的生成任务提供了新的理论框架，能简化学习难度、保证采样正确性，并取得更优的性能表现。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.

</details>


### [158] [Frequency-Forcing: From Scaling-as-Time to Soft Frequency Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20902)
*Weitao Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Frequency-Forcing 方法，通过轻量级可学习小波包变换从数据自身生成辅助低频流，以软机制实现频率排序生成，在不改变核心像素流路径的前提下提升图像生成质量，在 ImageNet-256 上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配模型引入显式生成顺序（先粗后细）对自然图像合成有效。K-Flow 通过硬频率约束实现，Latent Forcing 通过软机制实现。本文希望在不重写核心流坐标的前提下，利用软机制的灵活性实现硬频率约束的排序效果，并避免对外部预训练编码器的依赖。

Method: 提出 Frequency-Forcing，以 Latent Forcing 的软引导机制实现 K-Flow 的频率排序。核心创新是用轻量级可学习小波包变换从数据自身提取低频辅助流（自强制信号），代替 Latent Forcing 中依赖 DINO 等预训练编码器的 scratchpad，同时避免 K-Flow 中固定基底的局限性。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，Frequency-Forcing 在像素空间和潜在空间基线上均持续改进 FID 指标，并能与语义流自然结合以进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 基于强制机制的尺度排序是一种灵活的、保持路径不变的高效替代方案，Frequency-Forcing 证明了软机制可以实现硬频率约束的排序效果，且具有更好的泛用性和数据适应性。

Abstract: While standard flow-matching models transport noise to data uniformly, incorporating an explicit generation order - specifically, establishing coarse, low-frequency structure before fine detail - has proven highly effective for synthesizing natural images. Two recent works offer distinct paradigms for this. K-Flow imposes a hard frequency constraint by reinterpreting a frequency scaling variable as flow time, running the trajectory inside a transformed amplitude space. Latent Forcing provides a soft ordering mechanism by coupling the pixel flow with an auxiliary semantic latent flow via asynchronous time schedules, leaving the pixel interpolation path itself untouched. Viewed from the angle of improving pixel generation, we observe that forcing - guiding generation with an earlier-maturing auxiliary stream - offers a highly compatible route to scale-ordered generation without rewriting the core flow coordinate. Building on this, we propose Frequency-Forcing, which realizes K-Flow's frequency ordering through Latent Forcing's soft mechanism: a standard pixel flow is guided by an auxiliary low-frequency stream that matures earlier in time. Unlike Latent Forcing, whose scratchpad relies on a heavy pretrained encoder (e.g., DINO), our frequency scratchpad is derived from the data itself via a lightweight learnable wavelet packet transform. We term this a self-forcing signal, which avoids external dependencies while learning a basis better adapted to data statistics than the fixed bases used in hard frequency flows. On ImageNet-256, Frequency-Forcing consistently improves FID over strong pixel- and latent-space baselines, and naturally composes with a semantic stream to yield further gains. This illustrates that forcing-based scale ordering is a versatile, path-preserving alternative to hard frequency flows.

</details>


### [159] [Do Masked Autoencoders Improve Downhole Prediction? An Empirical Study on Real Well Drilling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20909)
*Aleksander Berezowski,Hassan Hassanzadeh,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练应用于钻井遥测指标预测，在Utah FORGE两口地热井约350万时间步的数据上进行了72种MAE配置的系统搜索，并与监督LSTM和GRU基线对比。最佳MAE相比GRU将测试MAE降低19.8%，但比LSTM高6.4%。潜空间宽度是主导架构因素（r=-0.59），而掩码比例影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 井下钻井遥测存在根本性的标签不对称问题：地面传感器数据以1Hz频率连续生成，而带标签的井下测量数据成本高昂、间歇性且稀缺。当前的机器学习方法普遍采用从头开始的完全监督训练，不适合这种数据分布。

Method: 使用两个公开的Utah FORGE地热井数据集（约350万时间步的多变量钻井遥测数据），对72种MAE配置进行全因子设计空间搜索，并与监督LSTM和GRU基线在预测Total Mud Volume任务上进行比较。

Result: 最佳MAE配置相比监督GRU基线将测试MAE降低19.8%，但比监督LSTM基线高6.4%。潜空间宽度是主导架构选择（Pearson r=-0.59），掩码比例影响可忽略，归因于1Hz钻井数据的高时间冗余性。

Conclusion: MAE预训练可作为钻井分析中可行的新范式，并确定了其最有益的条件。这一发现挑战了完全监督训练在该领域的默认地位。

Abstract: Downhole drilling telemetry presents a fundamental labeling asymmetry: surface sensor data are generated continuously at 1~Hz, while labeled downhole measurements are costly, intermittent, and scarce. Current machine learning approaches for downhole metric prediction universally adopt fully supervised training from scratch, which is poorly suited to this data regime. We present the first empirical evaluation of masked autoencoder (MAE) pretraining for downhole drilling metric prediction. Using two publicly available Utah FORGE geothermal wells comprising approximately 3.5 million timesteps of multivariate drilling telemetry, we conduct a systematic full-factorial design space search across 72 MAE configurations and compare them against supervised LSTM and GRU baselines on the task of predicting Total Mud Volume. Results show that the best MAE configuration reduces test mean absolute error by 19.8\% relative to the supervised GRU baseline, while trailing the supervised LSTM baseline by 6.4\%. Analysis of design dimensions reveals that latent space width is the dominant architectural choice (Pearson $r = -0.59$ with test MAE), while masking ratio has negligible effect, an unexpected finding attributed to high temporal redundancy in 1~Hz drilling data. These results establish MAE pretraining as a viable paradigm for drilling analytics and identify the conditions under which it is most beneficial.

</details>


### [160] [FairyFuse: Multiplication-Free LLM Inference on CPUs via Fused Ternary Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20913)
*Fei Zuo,Xiaoyan Xi,Quanyi Zeng,Feiyu Wang,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairyFuse是一个在CPU上实现三元权重LLM无乘法推理的系统，通过AVX-512掩码加减法融合子GEMV，在Intel Xeon 8558P上达到32.4 tokens/s，比llama.cpp Q4_K_M快1.24倍，质量接近无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在CPU-only平台部署时，内存带宽是自回归生成的主要瓶颈。虽然4比特或更低权重量化减少了内存压力，但现有系统仍需反量化权重并执行浮点乘法。三元权重{-1,0,+1}可将乘法替换为条件加减法或无操作，但已有系统未能利用这一结构优势。

Method: 提出FairyFuse推理系统，通过将每个广泛线性层的8个实值子GEMV融合为单个AVX-512循环，使用掩码加法和减法实现乘法无关执行。利用16倍权重压缩将内存受限的GEMV推向计算受限区域。

Result: 内核加速比29.6倍；在单Intel Xeon 8558P上达到32.4 tokens/s，比llama.cpp Q4_K_M快1.24倍；WikiText-2困惑度5.52 vs FP16的5.47，下游准确率66.0%，质量近无损。

Conclusion: 三元权重结构结合AVX-512掩码操作可有效解决CPU上LLM推理的内存带宽瓶颈，在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升吞吐量，但对GPU收益有限。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed on CPU-only platforms where memory bandwidth is the primary bottleneck for autoregressive generation. Weight quantization to four bits or below reduces memory pressure, yet existing systems still dequantize weights and perform floating-point multiplications, limiting the achievable gains. Ternary weights in {-1, 0, +1} provide a more efficient alternative, replacing multiplications with conditional additions, subtractions, or no-ops. While Fairy2i shows that ternary LLMs can match FP16 quality, its runtime does not exploit this structure. We present FairyFuse, an inference system that enables multiplication-free execution on commodity CPUs by fusing the eight real-valued sub-GEMVs of each widely-linear layer into a single AVX-512 loop using masked additions and subtractions, with zero floating-point multiplications. Roofline analysis shows that 16x weight compression shifts memory-bound GEMV toward the compute regime on bandwidth-limited CPUs, yielding a 29.6x kernel speedup while offering little benefit on GPUs. End-to-end, FairyFuse achieves 32.4 tokens per second on a single Intel Xeon 8558P, outperforming llama.cpp Q4_K_M by 1.24x with near-lossless quality (WikiText-2 perplexity 5.52 vs. 5.47 FP16; downstream accuracy 66.0%).

</details>


### [161] [Forget, Then Recall: Learnable Compression and Selective Unfolding via Gist Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20920)
*Yuzhen Mao,Michael Y. Li,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSA（Gist Sparse Attention），通过可学习的gist压缩令牌作为路由信号，实现粗到细的稀疏注意力机制，在保持端到端可训练性的同时，以对数级解码复杂度实现长上下文的高效处理，在LongBench和RAG基准上超越了多种压缩和稀疏注意力的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放大语言模型到长上下文面临全面注意力的二次计算成本问题，现有缓解方法包括KV缓存选择或压缩技术，但缺乏一种无需架构修改、端到端可学习的桥接方案。

Method: 提出选择性展开机制（GSA）：首先将上下文压缩为gist令牌，然后选择最相关的gist，再恢复对应的原始块进行细粒度注意力计算。通过端到端训练整合该过程，并通过递归gist-of-gist构建实现层次化扩展，达成对数级解码复杂度。

Result: 在LongBench和RAG基准上，在8倍到32倍的压缩比下，该方法一致优于其他压缩基线和推理时稀疏注意力方法。

Conclusion: 交错gist压缩令牌可以作为稀疏注意力的路由信号，实现高效的长上下文处理；该方法无需外部检索模块且不修改架构；层次化扩展支持多分辨率上下文访问。

Abstract: Scaling large language models to long contexts is challenging due to the quadratic computational cost of full attention. Mitigation approaches include KV-cache selection or compression techniques. We instead provide an effective and end-to-end learnable bridge between the two without requiring architecture modification. In particular, our key insight is that interleaved gist compression tokens -- which provide a learnable summary of sets of raw tokens -- can serve as routing signals for sparse attention. Building on this, we introduce selective unfolding via GSA, which first compresses the context into gist tokens, then selects the most relevant gists, and subsequently restores the corresponding raw chunks for detailed attention. This yields a simple coarse-to-fine mechanism that combines compact global representations with targeted access to fine-grained evidence. We further incorporate this process directly into training in an end-to-end fashion, avoiding the need for external retrieval modules. In addition, we extend the framework hierarchically via recursive gist-of-gist construction, enabling multi-resolution context access with logarithmic per-step decoding complexity. Empirical results on LongBench and RAG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms other compression baselines as well as inference-time sparse attention methods across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $32\times$. The code is available at: https://github.com/yuzhenmao/gist-sparse-attention/

</details>


### [162] [Validating a Deep Learning Algorithm to Identify Patients with Glaucoma using Systemic Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20921)
*John Xiang,Rohith Ravindranath,Sophia Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用All of Us国家数据训练的青光眼风险评估模型，仅通过系统电子健康记录（非眼科专科数据）即可在独立机构识别高风险患者，最佳模型AUROC达0.883，PPV为0.657，最高预测十分位数组青光眼诊断率65.7%、治疗率57.0%，表明无需专科影像即可实现可扩展、可及的青光眼预筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否仅利用全身性电子健康记录（而非专科眼科影像）即可训练出能有效识别高风险青光眼患者的风险评估模型，以实现更可扩展、可及的大规模预筛查。

Method: 基于All of Us国家数据预训练青光眼风险评估模型，在斯坦福大学独立队列（20,636名患者，15%患青光眼）上进行微调，使用人口统计学、全身诊断、用药、实验室检查和体格检查数据作为输入，并在留出测试集上评估模型性能。

Result: 最佳模型AUROC达0.883，阳性预测值（PPV）为0.657；最高预测十分位数组青光眼诊断率65.7%、治疗率57.0%；模型性能随可训练层数（最多15层）和数据量增加而提升。

Conclusion: 仅依赖电子健康记录的青光眼风险评估模型可在不依赖专科眼科影像的情况下实现可扩展、可及的青光眼预筛查，具有良好的性能与临床校准一致性。

Abstract: We evaluated whether a glaucoma risk assessment (GRA) model trained on All of Us national data can identify patients at high probability of glaucoma using only systemic electronic health records (EHR) at an independent institution. In this cross-sectional study, 20,636 Stanford patients seen from November 2013 to January 2024 were included (15% with glaucoma). A pretrained GRA model was fine-tuned on the Stanford cohort and tested on a held-out set using demographics, systemic diagnoses, medications, laboratory results, and physical examination measurements as inputs. The best model achieved AUROC 0.883 and PPV 0.657. Calibration was consistent with clinical risk: the highest prediction decile showed the greatest glaucoma diagnosis rate (65.7%) and treatment rate (57.0%). Performance improved with more trainable layers up to 15 and with additional data. An EHR-only GRA model may enable scalable and accessible pre-screening without specialized imaging.

</details>


### [163] [ILDR: Geometric Early Detection of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20923)
*Shreel Golwala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出类间/类内距离比(ILDR)作为Grokking现象的早期检测指标，该指标在表征空间几何重组阶段即可预警，比验证准确率突变提前9-73%的训练预算，并能作为早停触发器和优化器干预信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Grokking检测信号（权重范数、GrokFast梯度EMA）存在滞后或不稳定问题，需要一种更早期、稳定且具有几何意义的检测指标。

Method: 基于Fisher线性判别准则，计算倒数第二层表征的类间质心距离与类内散度之比（ILDR），阈值设为基线的2.5倍。复杂度O(|C|^2 + N)，仅在留出数据上计算以避免记忆化干扰。

Result: 在模算术和置换群(S5)任务上，ILDR比验证准确率突变提前9-73%；8个随机种子上领先950±250步，变异系数26%，Grokking后方差下降1696倍；作为早停触发器减少18.6%训练；优化器干预可双向控制转变。

Conclusion: ILDR追踪的是泛化背后的表征空间几何条件而非下游相关性，是Grokking转变的因果性而非相关性指标。

Abstract: Grokking describes a delayed generalization phenomenon in which a neural network achieves perfect training accuracy long before validation accuracy improves, followed by an abrupt transition to strong generalization. Existing detection signals are indirect: weight norm reflects parameter-space regularization and consistently lags the transition, while GrokFast's slow gradient EMA, used without gradient amplification, is unstable across seeds with standard deviation exceeding mean lead time. We propose the Inter/Intra-class Distance Ratio (ILDR), a geometric metric computed on second-to-last layer representations as the ratio of inter-class centroid separation to intra-class scatter. ILDR provides an early detection signal: it rises and crosses a threshold at 2.5 times its baseline before the grokking transition appears in validation accuracy, indicating early geometric reorganization in representation space. Grounded in Fisher's linear discriminant criterion, ILDR requires no eigendecomposition and runs in O(|C|^2 + N). It is evaluated exclusively on held-out data, making it robust to memorization effects. Across modular arithmetic and permutation group composition (S5), ILDR leads the grokking transition by 9 to 73 percent of the training budget, with lead time increasing with task algebraic complexity. Over eight random seeds, ILDR leads by 950 +/- 250 steps with a coefficient of variation of 26 percent, and post-grokking variance drops by 1696 times, consistent with a sharp phase transition in representation space. Using ILDR as an early stopping trigger reduces training by 18.6 percent on average. Optimizer interventions triggered at the ILDR threshold demonstrate bidirectional control over the transition, suggesting ILDR tracks representational conditions underlying generalization rather than a downstream correlate.

</details>


### [164] [Clinically Interpretable Sepsis Early Warning via LLM-Guided Simulation of Temporal Physiological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20924)
*Weizhi Nie,Zhen Qu,Weijie Wang,Chunpei Li,Ke Lu,Bingyang Zhou,Hongzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种大语言模型引导的时间模拟框架，用于脓毒症的早期可解释预警。该框架通过模拟生理轨迹实现透明预测，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上取得了0.861-0.903的AUC，优于传统深度学习和基于规则的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动的脓毒症早期预警模型虽然准确但缺乏可解释性，限制了临床医生的信任和应用。现有方法无法提供符合临床认知的预测机制，亟需一种既能保持高精度又能提供可解释生理轨迹的预警框架。

Method: 提出LLM引导的时间模拟框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 时空特征提取模块，捕捉多元生命体征的动态依赖关系；(2) Medical Prompt-as-Prefix模块，将临床推理线索嵌入LLM；(3) 基于智能体的后处理组件，将预测约束在生理学合理范围内。模型先模拟关键生理指标的演变，再进行脓毒症发作分类。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上，方法在24至4小时发疹前预测任务中取得0.861-0.903的AUC，优于传统深度学习和基于规则的方法。同时提供可解释的轨迹和风险趋势。

Conclusion: 该框架通过模拟生理轨迹实现了透明且可解释的脓毒症早期预警，性能优于现有方法，能够辅助临床医生进行早期干预和个性化决策。

Abstract: Timely and interpretable early warning of sepsis remains a major clinical challenge due to the complex temporal dynamics of physiological deterioration. Traditional data-driven models often provide accurate yet opaque predictions, limiting physicians' confidence and clinical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided temporal simulation framework that explicitly models physiological trajectories prior to disease onset for clinically interpretable prediction. The framework consists of a spatiotemporal feature extraction module that captures dynamic dependencies among multivariate vital signs, a Medical Prompt-as-Prefix module that embeds clinical reasoning cues into LLMs, and an agent-based post-processing component that constrains predictions within physiologically plausible ranges. By first simulating the evolution of key physiological indicators and then classifying sepsis onset, our model offers transparent prediction mechanisms that align with clinical judgment. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV and eICU databases, the proposed method achieves superior AUC scores (0.861-0.903) across 24-4-hour pre-onset prediction tasks, outperforming conventional deep learning and rule-based approaches. More importantly, it provides interpretable trajectories and risk trends that can assist clinicians in early intervention and personalized decision-making in intensive care environments.

</details>


### [165] [Domain-Aware Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for Semi-Supervised Generalization Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20928)
*Junyu Ren,Wensheng Gan,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAHCL框架，通过域感知学习（DAL）缓解伪标签跨域偏差，并结合层次对比学习（HCL）利用动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督提升未标记样本利用率，解决半监督域泛化故障诊断中伪标签偏差和样本利用不均问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督域泛化故障诊断方法存在两个耦合局限性：1）伪标签生成仅依赖标记源域知识，忽略域间几何差异导致系统性跨域偏差；2）对未标记样本采用硬接受或丢弃策略，刚性阈值导致跨域样本利用不平衡，硬标签分配易引入噪声。

Method: 提出域感知层次对比学习（DAHCL）统一框架，包含两个核心模块：1）域感知学习（DAL）模块显式捕获源域几何特征并校准跨异构源域的伪标签预测，缓解伪标签生成中的跨域偏差；2）层次对比学习（HCL）模块结合动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督，使不确定样本无需依赖不可靠硬标签即可贡献于表示学习。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了DAHCL的有效性，并在评估协议中引入了工程噪声以更好反映实际工业场景。

Conclusion: DAHCL通过联合提升监督质量和未标记样本利用率，有效解决了SSDGFD中伪标签偏差与样本利用不均问题，更贴近实际工业场景。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unseen operating conditions remains highly challenging when labeled data are scarce. Semi-supervised domain generalization fault diagnosis (SSDGFD) provides a practical solution by jointly exploiting labeled and unlabeled source domains. However, existing methods still suffer from two coupled limitations. First, pseudo-labels for unlabeled domains are typically generated primarily from knowledge learned on the labeled source domain, which neglects domain-specific geometric discrepancies and thus induces systematic cross-domain pseudo-label bias. Second, unlabeled samples are commonly handled with a hard accept-or-discard strategy, where rigid thresholding causes imbalanced sample utilization across domains, while hard-label assignment for uncertain samples can easily introduce additional noise. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework termed domain-aware hierarchical contrastive learning (DAHCL) for SSDGFD. Specifically, DAHCL introduces a domain-aware learning (DAL) module to explicitly capture source-domain geometric characteristics and calibrate pseudo-label predictions across heterogeneous source domains, thereby mitigating cross-domain bias in pseudo-label generation. In addition, DAHCL develops a hierarchical contrastive learning (HCL) module that combines dynamic confidence stratification with fuzzy contrastive supervision, enabling uncertain samples to contribute to representation learning without relying on unreliable hard labels. In this way, DAHCL jointly improves the quality of supervision and the utilization of unlabeled samples. Furthermore, to better reflect practical industrial scenarios, we incorporate engineering noise into the SSDGFD evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that...

</details>


### [166] [Data-Driven Open-Loop Simulation for Digital-Twin Operator Decision Support in Wastewater Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20935)
*Gary Simethy,Daniel Ortiz Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CCSS-RS模型，一种受控连续时间状态空间模型，用于污水处理厂数字孪生决策支持。该模型能处理不规则/缺失传感数据，在Avedøre基准上相比基线降低RMSE 40-46%，并通过四个案例研究验证了其在离线场景筛选中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水处理厂需要数字孪生决策支持工具，能够模拟控制计划下的工厂响应、容忍不规则和缺失传感数据、并在12-36小时规划范围内保持信息有效性，但用全规模工厂数据满足这些要求仍是工程-人工智能领域的开放挑战。

Method: 提出CCSS-RS（受控连续时间状态空间模型），将历史状态推断与未来控制和外部变量展开分离。结合类型化上下文编码、增益加权强制驱动、半群一致性展开，以及针对污水处理厂数据重尾和零膨胀特性的Student-t加障碍输出。

Result: 在Avedøre全规模基准（906,815时间步、43%缺失率、1-20分钟不规则采样）上，H=1000时RMSE 0.696、CRPS 0.349。相比Neural CDE基线降低RMSE 40-46%，相比简化内部变体降低31-35%。四个案例研究展示了实际运营价值。

Conclusion: CCSS-RS被确立为工业污水处理中离线场景筛选的实用学习模拟器，与机理模型互补。

Abstract: Wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) need digital-twin-style decision support tools that can simulate plant response under prescribed control plans, tolerate irregular and missing sensing, and remain informative over 12-36 h planning horizons. Meeting these requirements with full-scale plant data remains an open engineering-AI challenge. We present CCSS-RS, a controlled continuous-time state-space model that separates historical state inference from future control and exogenous rollout. The model combines typed context encoding, gain-weighted forcing of prescribed and forecast drivers, semigroup-consistent rollouts, and Student-t plus hurdle outputs for heavy-tailed and zero-inflated WWTP sensor data. On the public Avedøre full-scale benchmark, with 906,815 timesteps, 43% missingness, and 1-20 min irregular sampling, CCSS-RS achieves RMSE 0.696 and CRPS 0.349 at H=1000 across 10,000 test windows. This reduces RMSE by 40-46% relative to Neural CDE baselines and by 31-35% relative to simplified internal variants. Four case studies using a frozen checkpoint on test data demonstrate operational value: oxygen-setpoint perturbations shift predicted ammonium by -2.3 to +1.4 over horizons 300-1000; a smoothed setpoint plan ranks first in multi-criterion screening; context-only sensor outages raise monitored-variable RMSE by at most 10%; and ammonium, nitrate, and oxygen remain more accurate than persistence throughout the rollout. These results establish CCSS-RS as a practical learned simulator for offline scenario screening in industrial wastewater treatment, complementary to mechanistic models.

</details>


### [167] [Sink-Token-Aware Pruning for Fine-Grained Video Understanding in Efficient Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20937)
*Kibum Kim,Jiwan Kim,Kyle Min,Yueqi Wang,Jinyoung Moon,Julian McAuley,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有视频大语言模型因视觉token过多导致高推理延迟，剪枝方法在细粒度理解任务中性能骤降。本文发现"下沉token"（sink tokens）是罪魁祸首，并提出SToP方法通过下沉分数抑制这些token，在剪枝90%视觉token时仍显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的视觉token剪枝方法在MCQA基准上表现良好，但在需要精确视觉定位的细粒度任务（如幻觉评估）上性能严重下降，亟需解决这一差距。

Method: 提出Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP)，引入下沉分数（sink score）量化每个token成为下沉token的倾向性，将其应用于现有空间和时间剪枝方法中抑制下沉token，从而提升视频理解能力。

Result: 将SToP应用于VisionZip、FastVid、Holitom等最新剪枝方法，在幻觉评估、开放式生成、组合推理和MCQA等多个基准上显著提升性能，即使剪枝90%视觉token仍有效。

Conclusion: 下沉token是阻碍细粒度视频理解的关键因素，SToP作为即插即用方法能有效抑制下沉token，大幅提升高剪枝率下的视频理解性能。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) incur high inference latency due to a large number of visual tokens provided to LLMs. To address this, training-free visual token pruning has emerged as a solution to reduce computational costs; however, existing methods are primarily validated on Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) benchmarks, where coarse-grained cues often suffice. In this work, we reveal that these methods suffer a sharp performance collapse on fine-grained understanding tasks requiring precise visual grounding, such as hallucination evaluation. To explore this gap, we conduct a systematic analysis and identify sink tokens--semantically uninformative tokens that attract excessive attention--as a key obstacle to fine-grained video understanding. When these sink tokens survive pruning, they distort the model's visual evidence and hinder fine-grained understanding. Motivated by these insights, we propose Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP), a simple yet effective plug-and-play method that introduces a sink score to quantify each token's tendency to behave as a sink and applies this score to existing spatial and temporal pruning methods to suppress them, thereby enhancing video understanding. To validate the effectiveness of SToP, we apply it to state-of-the-art pruning methods (VisionZip, FastVid, and Holitom) and evaluate it across diverse benchmarks covering hallucination, open-ended generation, compositional reasoning, and MCQA. Our results demonstrate that SToP significantly boosts performance, even when pruning up to 90% of visual tokens.

</details>


### [168] [SCM: Sleep-Consolidated Memory with Algorithmic Forgetting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20943)
*Saish Sachin Shinde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCM是一种受神经科学启发的LLM记忆架构，通过工作记忆、重要性标记、睡眠阶段巩固（NREM/REM）、基于价值的遗忘和自我模型等五个核心组件，实现了十轮对话的完美召回率、90.9%的记忆噪声降低以及亚毫秒级搜索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM缺乏持久、结构化且生物上合理的记忆机制，现有方法（上下文窗口截断、无界向量数据库、分层存储）缺少巩固和遗忘机制，无法有效模拟人类记忆系统。

Method: 提出SCM架构，包含五个核心组件：有限容量工作记忆、多维重要性标记、离线睡眠阶段巩固（区分NREM和REM阶段）、基于意图的价值驱动遗忘、以及支持内省的计算自我模型。

Result: 在八项标准化基准测试中，十轮对话达到完美召回率，自适应遗忘将记忆噪声降低90.9%，数百个概念存储下搜索延迟低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: 该工作为具备巩固、优先级排序和遗忘能力的记忆系统奠定了架构基础，为推进LLM记忆研究提供了可测试的平台。

Abstract: We present SCM (Sleep-Consolidated Memory), a research preview of a memory architecture for large language models that draws on neuroscientific principles to address a fundamental limitation in current systems: the absence of persistent, structured, and biologically plausible memory. Existing approaches rely on truncating context windows, growing vector databases without bound, or tiered storage systems that lack consolidation and forgetting mechanisms. SCM implements five core components inspired by human memory: a limited-capacity working memory, multi-dimensional importance tagging, offline sleep-stage consolidation with distinct NREM and REM phases, intentional value-based forgetting, and a computational self-model enabling introspection. Across a standardized benchmark suite of eight tests, the prototype achieves perfect recall accuracy over ten-turn conversations while reducing memory noise by 90.9% through adaptive forgetting. Memory search latency remains below one millisecond even with hundreds of stored concepts. This work establishes the architectural foundations for memory systems that consolidate, prioritize, and forget, offering a testable platform for advancing LLM memory research.

</details>


### [169] [LAF-Based Evaluation and UTTL-Based Learning Strategies with MIATTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20944)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EL-MIATT框架下两种互补机制：基于LAF的评估算法和基于UTTL的学习策略，用于在真实目标不确定的机器学习场景中实现逻辑一致且实际可行的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际机器学习应用中，真实目标由于模糊性或主观信息而无法精确定义，传统依赖客观"真实标签"的监督学习方法在此类场景下失效，需要一种在不假设真实目标客观存在的前提下进行评估和学习的框架。

Method: 1) 分析任务特定MIATT的覆盖率和多样性对其结构属性及下游评估学习的影响；2) 提出基于LAF的评估算法，可在原始MIATT或由其合成的三元目标上运行；3) 引入基于UTTL的学习策略，使用Dice和交叉熵损失函数，比较逐目标与聚合优化方案；4) 讨论LAF与UTTL的整合如何桥接逻辑语义与统计优化。

Result: 构建了LAF评估算法与UTTL学习策略的互补机制，实现了在不确定监督下的逻辑连贯且实际可行的建模，并提供了理论到实践的完整路径。

Conclusion: 该工作为在"真实标签"本质不确定的场景下开发机器学习系统提供了原则性基础，通过在多个不准确真实目标间实现逻辑一致的评估与学习，弥合了逻辑语义与统计优化之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: In many real-world machine learning (ML) applications, the true target cannot be precisely defined due to ambiguity or subjectivity information. To address this challenge, under the assumption that the true target for a given ML task is not assumed to exist objectively in the real world, the EL-MIATTs (Evaluation and Learning with Multiple Inaccurate True Targets) framework has been proposed. Bridging theory and practice in implementing EL-MIATTs, in this paper, we develop two complementary mechanisms: LAF (Logical Assessment Formula)-based evaluation algorithms and UTTL (Undefinable True Target Learning)-based learning strategies with MIATTs, which together enable logically coherent and practically feasible modeling under uncertain supervision. We first analyze task-specific MIATTs, examining how their coverage and diversity determine their structural property and influence downstream evaluation and learning. Based on this understanding, we formulate LAF-grounded evaluation algorithms that operate either on original MIATTs or on ternary targets synthesized from them, balancing interpretability, soundness, and completeness. For model training, we introduce UTTL-grounded learning strategies using Dice and cross-entropy loss functions, comparing per-target and aggregated optimization schemes. We also discuss how the integration of LAF and UTTL bridges the gap between logical semantics and statistical optimization. Together, these components provide a coherent pathway for implementing EL-MIATTs, offering a principled foundation for developing ML systems in scenarios where the notion of "ground truth" is inherently uncertain. An application of this work's results is presented as part of the study available at https://www.qeios.com/read/EZWLSN.

</details>


### [170] [SGD at the Edge of Stability: The Stochastic Sharpness Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21016)
*Fangshuo Liao,Afroditi Kolomvaki,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机自稳定理论，将自稳定框架扩展到小批量SGD，揭示了梯度噪声通过沿着顶部Hessian特征向量的振荡动力学注入方差，增强了三次方锐度降低力，使平衡点降至2/η以下。推导出闭式平衡锐度缺口公式ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，预测更小的批量产生更平坦的解，并在全批量时恢复GD行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GD训练中，锐度上升到2/η并停滞（EoS现象），已由三次结构驱动的自稳定机制解释。但对于小批量SGD，锐度稳定在2/η以下且随批量减小缺口扩大，尚无理论解释。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将自稳定框架扩展到SGD，引入随机自稳定概念。关键洞察是梯度噪声沿顶部Hessian特征向量注入方差，增强三次方锐度减少力，改变平衡点。定义相对于移动投影梯度下降轨迹的随机预测动力学，证明随机耦合定理约束SGD与预测的偏差，推导出闭式平衡锐度缺口公式。

Result: 推导出闭式平衡锐度缺口ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，其中α是渐进锐化率，β是自稳定强度，σ_u²是投影到顶部特征向量的梯度噪声方差。该公式预测更小批量产生更平坦的解，全批量时恢复GD结果。

Conclusion: 成功将自稳定理论扩展到SGD，为小批量训练中锐度抑制现象提供了理论解释，建立了批量大小与解平坦性之间的定量关系。

Abstract: When training neural networks with full-batch gradient descent (GD) and step size $η$, the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian -- the sharpness $S(\boldsymbolθ)$ -- rises to $2/η$ and hovers there, a phenomenon termed the Edge of Stability (EoS). \citet{damian2023selfstab} showed that this behavior is explained by a self-stabilization mechanism driven by third-order structure of the loss, and that GD implicitly follows projected gradient descent (PGD) on the constraint $ S(\boldsymbolθ)\leq 2/η$. For mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD), the sharpness stabilizes below $2/η$, with the gap widening as the batch size decreases; yet no theoretical explanation exists for this suppression.
  We introduce stochastic self-stabilization, extending the self-stabilization framework to SGD. Our key insight is that gradient noise injects variance into the oscillatory dynamics along the top Hessian eigenvector, strengthening the cubic sharpness-reducing force and shifting the equilibrium below $2/η$. Following the approach of \citet{damian2023selfstab}, we define stochastic predicted dynamics relative to a moving projected gradient descent trajectory and prove a stochastic coupling theorem that bounds the deviation of SGD from these predictions. We derive a closed-form equilibrium sharpness gap: $ΔS = ηβσ_{\boldsymbol{u}}^{2}/(4α)$, where $α$ is the progressive sharpening rate, $β$ is the self-stabilization strength, and $σ_{ \boldsymbol{u}}^{2}$ is the gradient noise variance projected onto the top eigenvector. This formula predicts that smaller batch sizes yield flatter solutions and recovers GD when the batch equals the full dataset.

</details>


### [171] [MCAP: Deployment-Time Layer Profiling for Memory-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21026)
*Anurita Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCAP（蒙特卡洛激活剖析）方法，在模型加载时评估每层重要性，据此动态调整精度（W4A8 vs W4A16）和内存层级（GPU/RAM/SSD），使NVE系统在异构硬件上实现1.5-1.8倍解码吞吐提升，且无需修改权重即可在以往不可行的内存预算下运行模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署到异构硬件时，性能瓶颈往往来自内存而非计算，需要在有限且多样化的内存预算下高效运行模型。

Method: 提出MCAP（蒙特卡洛激活剖析），一种加载时逐层重要性估计器；基于该信号进行精度调度（W4A8 vs W4A16）和驻留层级决策（GPU、RAM、SSD），无需修改权重即可适应不同内存预算。

Result: 在NVIDIA T4上，相比llama.cpp Q4_0，解码吞吐量提升1.5-1.8倍，且能在之前不可行的内存条件下运行模型。

Conclusion: MCAP与NVE系统有效缓解了LLM部署中的内存瓶颈，通过加载时动态精度与内存调度实现了显著性能提升和更广泛的内存适配性。

Abstract: Deploying large language models to heterogeneous hardware is often constrained by memory, not compute. We introduce MCAP (Monte Carlo Activation Profiling), a load-time per-layer importance estimator that enables dynamic precision and memory placement decisions on the target device. MCAP produces a lightweight per-layer signal that drives both precision dispatch (W4A8 vs. W4A16) and residency tier (GPU, RAM, SSD), allowing a single set of weights to operate across diverse memory budgets. Our system, NVE, achieves 1.5-1.8x higher decode throughput than llama.cpp Q4_0 on NVIDIA T4 and enables models to run in memory regimes previously infeasible without modifying weights.

</details>


### [172] [A Deep U-Net Framework for Flood Hazard Mapping Using Hydraulic Simulations of the Wupper Catchment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21028)
*Christian Lammers,Fernando Arévalo,Leonie Märker-Neuhaus,Daniel Heinenberg,Christian Förster,Karl-Heinz Spies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习（U-Net架构）的替代模型，用于快速、高效预测网格上的最大水位，作为传统计算密集型水动力模拟的替代方案，并在德国伍珀河流域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球洪涝事件的频率和严重性不断增加，亟需快速可靠的洪水预测工具，而传统水动力模拟计算成本高昂，难以满足快速预测的需求。

Method: 通过一系列实验优化U-Net架构、图像块生成策略和数据处理方式，构建深度学习替代模型来近似水动力模型，预测网格上的最大水位。

Result: 该深度学习替代模型在德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦州伍珀河流域的水动力模拟测试中，获得了与传统模拟方法可比拟的结果。

Conclusion: 深度学习替代模型可以作为传统水动力模拟的计算高效替代方案，在保证预测精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: The increasing frequency and severity of global flood events highlights the need for the development of rapid and reliable flood prediction tools. This process traditionally relies on computationally expensive hydraulic simulations. This research presents a prediction tool by developing a deep-learning based surrogate model to accurately and efficiently predict the maximum water level across a grid. This was achieved by conducting a series of experiments to optimize a U-Net architecture, patch generation, and data handling for approximating a hydraulic model. This research demonstrates that a deep learning surrogate model can serve as a computationally efficient alternative to traditional hydraulic simulations. The framework was tested using hydraulic simulations of the Wupper catchment in the North-Rhein Westphalia region (Germany), obtaining comparable results.

</details>


### [173] [Interpretable Quantile Regression by Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21042)
*Valentin Lemaire,Gaël Aglin,Siegfried Nijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法，该方法能提供目标变量完整条件分布的预测、具有可解释性，并且在不牺牲算法效率的前提下学习一组最优树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习领域对既准确又可解释且稳健的模型需求日益增长，以使最终用户能够理解和信任AI系统。现有方法在预测完整条件分布、保持可解释性和算法效率之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出了一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法。该方法无需对目标分布做先验假设即可预测完整条件分布，同时保持预测的可解释性，并且在算法效率上与学习单棵树相当。

Result: 该方法能同时满足三个优势：(1) 无先验分布假设下预测完整条件分布；(2) 预测结果具有可解释性；(3) 学习一组最优分位数回归树而不牺牲算法效率。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器学习中准确性、可解释性和稳健性的权衡提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过最优分位数回归树集合实现了高效且可解释的条件分布预测。

Abstract: The field of machine learning is subject to an increasing interest in models that are not only accurate but also interpretable and robust, thus allowing their end users to understand and trust AI systems. This paper presents a novel method for learning a set of optimal quantile regression trees. The advantages of this method are that (1) it provides predictions about the complete conditional distribution of a target variable without prior assumptions on this distribution; (2) it provides predictions that are interpretable; (3) it learns a set of optimal quantile regression trees without compromising algorithmic efficiency compared to learning a single tree.

</details>


### [174] [JEPAMatch: Geometric Representation Shaping for Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21046)
*Ali Aghababaei-Harandi,Aude Sportisse,Massih-Reza Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LeJEPA潜在空间正则化与FlexMatch的半监督学习方法，通过显式塑造几何表征（促使潜在空间呈各向同性高斯分布）来解决FixMatch类方法中存在的类别偏差和早期伪标签噪声问题，在CIFAR-100、STL-10和Tiny-ImageNet上取得了优于基线的方法，并显著加速了收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FixMatch类半监督方法存在两大瓶颈：多数类主导学习过程（被错误伪标签放大）导致模型偏差，以及早期噪声伪标签阻碍清晰决策边界的形成，需要长时间训练才能学到有效表征。

Method: 提出一种新的训练目标，将FlexMatch（FixMatch的自适应扩展）中的经典半监督损失与基于LeJEPA的潜在空间正则化项相结合。LeJEPA框架认为有意义的表征应在潜在空间中呈现各向同性高斯结构，据此显式塑造表征的几何形状。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、STL-10和Tiny-ImageNet上的大量实验表明，所提方法一致优于现有基线方法，同时显著加速了收敛，大幅降低了相比标准FixMatch管线的总体计算成本。

Conclusion: 从传统的逻辑输出阈值范式转向显式的几何表征塑造是有效的，潜在空间正则化能够鼓励良好结构的表征，同时保留伪标签策略的优势，实现更高效、更准确的半监督学习。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for leveraging large amounts of unlabeled data to improve the performance of machine learning models when labeled data are scarce. Among existing approaches, methods derived from FixMatch have achieved state-of-the-art results in image classification by combining weak and strong data augmentations with confidence-based pseudo-labeling. Despite their strong empirical performance, these methods typically struggle with two critical bottlenecks: majority classes tend to dominate the learning process, which is amplified by incorrect pseudo-labels, leading to biased models. Furthermore, noisy early pseudo-labels prevent the model from forming clear decision boundaries, requiring prolonged training to learn informative representation. In this paper, we introduce a paradigm shift from conventional logical output threshold base, toward an explicit shaping of geometric representations. Our approach is inspired by the recently proposed Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (LeJEPA), a theoretically grounded framework asserting that meaningful representations should exhibit an isotropic Gaussian structure in latent space. Building on this principle, we propose a new training objective that combines the classical semi-supervised loss used in FlexMatch, an adaptive extension of FixMatch, with a latent-space regularization term derived from LeJEPA.
  Our proposed approach, encourages well-structured representations while preserving the advantages of pseudo-labeling strategies. Through extensive experiments on CIFAR-100, STL-10 and Tiny-ImageNet, we demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing baselines. In addition, our method significantly accelerates the convergence, drastically reducing the overall computational cost compared to standard FixMatch-based pipelines.

</details>


### [175] [TRAVELFRAUDBENCH: A Configurable Evaluation Framework for GNN Fraud Ring Detection in Travel Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21093)
*Bhavana Sajja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TravelFraudBench (TFG) 是一个可配置的基准测试，用于在旅行平台图中评估GNN检测欺诈团伙的能力。它模拟了三种旅行特定欺诈团伙类型，包含9种节点类型和12种边类型。实验表明GraphSAGE（AUC=0.992）和RGCN-proj（AUC=0.987）表现最佳，HAN（AUC=0.935）与MLP基线持平。图结构显著提升了判别能力，设备与IP共现是主要信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（YelpChi、Amazon-Fraud、Elliptic、PaySim）仅覆盖单节点类型或领域通用模式，缺乏评估结构各异欺诈团伙拓扑的机制，无法满足旅行平台中复杂欺诈团伙检测需求。

Method: TFG模拟三种旅行特定欺诈团伙：票务欺诈（星型拓扑）、幽灵酒店方案（二分图团簇）、账户盗用链（忠诚度转移链）。构建含9节点类型、12边类型的异构图，支持团伙大小、数量、欺诈率、规模（500-200,000节点）等参数完全可配置。采用基于团伙的划分方式避免标签泄漏，评估了MLP、GraphSAGE、RGCN-proj、HAN、RGCN和PC-GNN六种方法。

Result: GraphSAGE AUC=0.992，RGCN-proj AUC=0.987，比MLP基线（0.938）分别高5.5和5.0个百分点。HAN（0.935）为负结果，与MLP持平。团伙恢复任务中，GraphSAGE达100%恢复率，MLP仅17-88%。边类型消融实验显示设备与IP共现是主要信号，移除uses_device导致AUC下降5.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 图结构对欺诈团伙检测具有显著判别力，但并非所有GNN方法都有效（HAN表现不佳）。设备与IP共现是关键特征。TFG作为开源可配置基准，为旅行平台欺诈检测研究提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: We introduce TravelFraudBench (TFG), a configurable benchmark for evaluating graph neural networks (GNNs) on fraud ring detection in travel platform graphs. Existing benchmarks--YelpChi, Amazon-Fraud, Elliptic, PaySim--cover single node types or domain-generic patterns with no mechanism to evaluate across structurally distinct fraud ring topologies. TFG simulates three travel-specific ring types--ticketing fraud (star topology with shared device/IP clusters), ghost hotel schemes (reviewer x hotel bipartite cliques), and account takeover rings (loyalty transfer chains)--in a heterogeneous graph with 9 node types and 12 edge types. Ring size, count, fraud rate, scale (500 to 200,000 nodes), and composition are fully configurable. We evaluate six methods--MLP, GraphSAGE, RGCN-proj, HAN, RGCN, and PC-GNN--under a ring-based split where each ring appears entirely in one partition, eliminating transductive label leakage. GraphSAGE achieves AUC=0.992 and RGCN-proj AUC=0.987, outperforming the MLP baseline (AUC=0.938) by 5.5 and 5.0 pp, confirming graph structure adds substantial discriminative power. HAN (AUC=0.935) is a negative result, matching the MLP baseline. On the ring recovery task (>=80% of ring members flagged simultaneously), GraphSAGE achieves 100% recovery across all ring types; MLP recovers only 17-88%. The edge-type ablation shows device and IP co-occurrence are the primary signals: removing uses_device drops AUC by 5.2 pp. TFG is released as an open-source Python package (MIT license) with PyG, DGL, and NetworkX exporters and pre-generated datasets at https://huggingface.co/datasets/bsajja7/travel-fraud-graphs, with Croissant metadata including Responsible AI fields.

</details>


### [176] [Spectral Embeddings Leak Graph Topology: Theory, Benchmark, and Adaptive Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21094)
*Thinh Nguyen-Cong,Truong-Son Hy,Thang N. Dinh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoGraB基准测试框架，用于模拟图数据碎片化场景，并设计AFR自适应重建方法，在9个数据集上验证了其有效性，同时具备差分隐私保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN基准测试假设图数据全局集中可用，但实际场景（联邦学习、分布式系统、隐私敏感应用）中图数据是局部化、碎片化、带噪声且存在隐私泄露风险的，缺乏统一的评估框架来反映这些现实挑战。

Method: 提出LoGraB框架，通过三种策略和四个控制变量（邻域半径d、谱质量k、噪声水平σ、覆盖比率p）将标准数据集分解为碎片化基准。提出AFR方法，利用保真度度量（结合间隙-截断稳定性比和结构熵）评估补丁质量，采用RANSAC-Procrustes对齐、自适应拼接和Bundle Adjustment进行碎片组装，并证明热核边恢复、Davis-Kahan扰动稳定性和有界对齐误差等理论保证。

Result: 在9个基准数据集上，LoGraB揭示了不同模型在碎片化下的优劣势；AFR在7/9数据集上取得最佳F1分数；在(ε,δ)-高斯差分隐私下，ε=2时AFR仍能保持未防御状态下75%的F1性能。

Conclusion: 碎片化图数据场景需要专门的方法，AFR通过保真度驱动的自适应重建能够有效处理噪声碎片，同时兼具隐私保护能力，为实际分布式图学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) excel on relational data, but standard benchmarks unrealistically assume the graph is centrally available. In practice, settings such as Federated Graph Learning, distributed systems, and privacy-sensitive applications involve graph data that are localized, fragmented, noisy, and privacy-leaking. We present a unified framework for this setting. We introduce LoGraB (Local Graph Benchmark), which decomposes standard datasets into fragmented benchmarks using three strategies and four controls: neighborhood radius $d$, spectral quality $k$, noise level $σ$, and coverage ratio $p$. LoGraB supports graph reconstruction, localized node classification, and inter-fragment link prediction, with Island Cohesion. We propose AFR (Adaptive Fidelity-driven Reconstruction), a method for noisy spectral fragments. AFR scores patch quality via a fidelity measure combining a gap-to-truncation stability ratio and structural entropy, then assembles fragments using RANSAC-Procrustes alignment, adaptive stitching, and Bundle Adjustment. Rather than forcing a single global graph, AFR recovers large faithful islands. We prove heat-kernel edge recovery under a separation condition, Davis--Kahan perturbation stability, and bounded alignment error. We establish a Spectral Leakage Proposition: under a spectral-gap assumption, polynomial-time Bayesian recovery is feasible once enough eigenvectors are shared, complementing AFR's deterministic guarantees. Experiments on nine benchmarks show that LoGraB reveals model strengths and weaknesses under fragmentation, AFR achieves the best F1 on 7/9 datasets, and under per-embedding $(ε,δ)$-Gaussian differential privacy, AFR retains 75% of its undefended F1 at $ε=2$. Our anonymous code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/JMLR_submission

</details>


### [177] [Preconditioned DeltaNet: Curvature-aware Sequence Modeling for Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21100)
*Neehal Tumma,Noel Loo,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现有delta规则递归（如DeltaNet、GDN、KDA）在测试时回归（TTR）框架中忽略最小二乘损失曲率的问题，提出引入预条件（preconditioning）技术。从在线最小二乘理论出发，推导了线性注意力与delta规则在精确预条件情况下的等价性，并通过对角近似实现实际应用，提出了预条件化的DeltaNet、GDN和KDA及其高效分块并行算法。实验表明，预条件化delta规则递归在合成回忆基准和340M/1B规模语言建模中均取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有delta规则递归被解释为测试时回归（TTR）框架中在线最小二乘更新的一阶近似，但它们忽略了最小二乘损失函数的曲率信息（即Hessian矩阵），导致优化效率不足。作者旨在通过引入预条件技术来弥补这一理论空白，从而提升递归算子的性能。

Method: 1. 从在线最小二乘理论出发，推导了线性注意力与delta规则在精确预条件情况下的等价关系。2. 提出对角近似预条件，将理论转化为实际可用的递归更新形式。3. 基于对角预条件，设计了预条件化的DeltaNet、GDN和KDA变体，并开发了相应的分块并行（chunkwise parallel）高效计算算法。

Result: 在合成回忆基准测试和340M/1B参数规模的语言建模任务上，预条件化的delta规则递归相比原始版本取得了一致的性能提升，证明了引入曲率信息的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入对角预条件来近似最小二乘损失的曲率，可以显著改进现有delta规则递归算子的表达能力与优化效率，为设计更高效的次二次长上下文注意力算子提供了新方向。

Abstract: To address the increasing long-context compute limitations of softmax attention, several subquadratic recurrent operators have been developed. This work includes models such as Mamba-2, DeltaNet, Gated DeltaNet (GDN), and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA). As the space of recurrences grows, a parallel line of work has arisen to taxonomize them. One compelling view is the test-time regression (TTR) framework, which interprets recurrences as performing online least squares updates that learn a linear map from the keys to values. Existing delta-rule recurrences can be seen as first-order approximations to this objective, but notably ignore the curvature of the least-squares loss during optimization. In this work, we address this by introducing preconditioning to these recurrences. Starting from the theory of online least squares, we derive equivalences between linear attention and the delta rule in the exactly preconditioned case. Next, we realize this theory in practice by proposing a diagonal approximation: this enables us to introduce preconditioned variants of DeltaNet, GDN, and KDA alongside efficient chunkwise parallel algorithms for computing them. Empirically, we find that our preconditioned delta-rule recurrences yield consistent performance improvements across synthetic recall benchmarks and language modeling at the 340M and 1B scale.

</details>


### [178] [How Much Is One Recurrence Worth? Iso-Depth Scaling Laws for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21106)
*Kristian Schwethelm,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过116次预训练实验，发现循环语言模型中每增加一次循环（深度循环）相当于增加约$r^{0.46}$个等效参数，即$\varphi=0.46$，介于完全等效($\varphi=1$)和无增益($\varphi=0$)之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化循环语言模型中额外循环次数与等效唯一参数之间的换算关系，为循环深度设计提供可预测的验证损失代价。

Method: 对循环次数$r \in \{1,2,4,8\}$的116次预训练实验进行等深度扫描，拟合联合缩放定律$L = E + A\,(N_\text{once} + r^{\varphi} N_\text{rec})^{-α} + B\,D^{-β}$，恢复出循环等效指数$\varphi$。

Result: $\varphi = 0.46$（$R^2=0.997$）；$r=4$时410M循环模型性能与580M非循环模型相当，但训练成本相当于1B非循环模型；下游任务中参数知识任务存在差距，简单开卷任务差距消失，推理任务在计算预算内无法解决。

Conclusion: $\varphi$可将循环次数$r$的设计选择转化为可预测的验证损失代价，未来训练方法和架构可通过能否将$\varphi$提升至$0.46$以上进行比较。

Abstract: We measure how much one extra recurrence is worth to a looped (depth-recurrent) language model, in equivalent unique parameters. From an iso-depth sweep of 116 pretraining runs across recurrence counts $r \in \{1, 2, 4, 8\}$ spanning ${\sim}50\times$ in training compute, we fit a joint scaling law $L = E + A\,(N_\text{once} + r^{\varphi} N_\text{rec})^{-α} + B\,D^{-β}$ and recover a new recurrence-equivalence exponent $\varphi = 0.46$ at $R^2 = 0.997$. Intuitively, $\varphi$ tells us whether looping a block $r$ times is equivalent in validation loss to $r$ unique blocks of a non-looped model (full equivalence, $\varphi{=}1$) or to a single block run repeatedly with no capacity gain ($\varphi{=}0$). Our $\varphi = 0.46$ sits in between, so each additional recurrence predictably increases validation loss at matched training compute. For example, at $r{=}4$ a 410M looped model performs on par with a 580M non-looped model, but pays the training cost of a 1B non-looped one. On a five-axis downstream evaluation, the gap persists on parametric-knowledge tasks and closes on simple open-book tasks, while reasoning tasks are not resolvable at our compute budgets. For any looped LM, our $\varphi$ converts the design choice of $r$ into a predictable validation-loss cost, and future training recipes and architectures can be compared by how much they raise $\varphi$ above $0.46$.

</details>


### [179] [Toward Efficient Membership Inference Attacks against Federated Large Language Models: A Projection Residual Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21197)
*Guilin Deng,Silong Chen,Yuchuan Luo,Yi Liu,Songlei Wang,Zhiping Cai,Lin Liu,Xiaohua Jia,Shaojing Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProjRes，一种针对联邦大语言模型（FedLLMs）的被动成员推理攻击方法，通过投影残差分析嵌入向量与梯度子空间的内在关联，无需影子模型或辅助分类器，在四个基准和四个LLM上实现近100%的攻击准确率，揭示了FedLLMs中此前被忽视的隐私漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦大语言模型虽通过数据本地化保护隐私，但共享梯度仍可能通过成员推理攻击泄露敏感信息；然而FedLLMs的大参数量、快速收敛、稀疏非正交梯度等特性使现有MIA方法失效，亟需针对性的攻击方案以评估其隐私风险。
